> DEVELOPER

接手 /Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/slides/HUSTopening.tex 和 /Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/slides/HUSTopening.pdf，重做本科毕业答辩 PPT 视觉设计。

目标：不是小修，而是重新建立一套更美观、对齐严格、投影清晰的 LaTeX Beamer 答辩 PPT。必须基于 HUSTopening / Madrid 模板，不要丢模板：目录、页脚、页码、标题栏、字体体系要保留或合理贴合模板。

当前版本共 18 页，内容主线是：
研究背景与意义 → 研究内容与技术路线 → 实验结果与分析 → 总结与展望。
核心叙事：真实道路攻击测试成本高且风险大，因此用 CARLA 仿真建立定位链路攻防闭环；先做静态攻击边界搜索，再验证防护开启后的变化，最后说明动态攻击与防护评价不能只看检测率。

关键事实不能改：
- 54 个有效场景实例
- 7 张 CARLA 地图
- 8 类道路模板
- 固定步长 0.05s
- 静态防护回放：15.4%→0.0%，23.1%→15.4%，25.0%→25.0%
- 防护触发均值：926 次、322 次、0 次
- 动态攻击：99.4%、50.1%、13.8%，其中 13.8% 是屏蔽诊断维度的补充设置，不是主结论
- 角速率 CUSUM：77 次闭环测试中，对预设 IMU 姿态攻击均于 5 秒内触发

当前版本的主要问题：
- 第 4 页、第 10 页、第 15 页、第 16 页被视觉审查判定不通过。
- 整体像“默认模板 + 卡片堆叠”，缺少设计感。
- 大量模块只是浅蓝色卡片，节奏单调。
- 部分页文字仍偏多，视觉焦点不够强。
- 图表页不够高级，第 10 页尤其需要重做。
- 第 15 页“防护效果与代价”应做成强结论页，不要三块普通文字卡片。
- 第 16 页总结应有主次层级，不要平均分配三块卡片。

106	        \small
107	        \begin{itemize}
108	          \tightlist
109	          \item 定位攻击测试具有主动扰动性质。
110	          \item 真实道路大规模重复测试成本高、周期长。
111	          \item 攻击测试本身会引入额外安全风险。

113	      \end{block}
114	    \end{column}

121	          \item 重复运行同一攻击与防护策略。
122	          \item 批量比较闭环结果和安全代价。
123	        \end{itemize}
124	      \end{block}

192	    \begin{enumerate}
193	      \tightlist
194	      \item 不同道路场景下，静态定位攻击的有效边界如何变化？
195	      \item 防护开启后，静态攻击参数是否仍能代表风险水平？
196	      \item 动态攻击策略是否会改变防护开启后的风险评估？
197	    \end{enumerate}
198	  \end{block}

/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/conclusion.tex:RQ2（动态隐蔽攻击）说明，逐场景静态最优不能代表防护开启后的真实威胁。在 13 个场景的统一回放中，静态参数在 GNSS 统计防护下严重失效率仅为 0\%--25\%，其中 GNSS 类攻击几乎全程被卡方检验和 CUSUM 拒绝。静态搜索结果更适合作为幅度先验和对照基准，不能替代防护开启后的动态评估。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/conclusion.tex:在 4 个路口场景的防护开启训练中，PPO 将检测率从 99.4\% 降至跨种子均值 50.1\%（最佳种子 30\%），并在训练末期保持 100\% 严重失效率。逐回合分析显示，策略主要形成了持续微小注入和隐蔽-爆发切换两类行为。屏蔽诊断维度并重新训练后，策略检测率降至 13.8\%；在 11 个跨地形场景零样本评估和跨速度部署中，策略也未出现明显退化。有限 CARLA 在线交互预算下，PPO 形成了低检测率策略，而 SAC 未观察到相同现象；算法优劣仍不能外推到一般任务。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/conclusion.tex:角速率残差监测提供了一个有限补充。在 77 个闭环测试中，角速率 CUSUM 对三类道路地形上的六类预设 IMU 姿态攻击均能在 5 秒内触发检测。因此，该结果应解释为检测覆盖增强，而不是完整防护能力证明。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/conclusion.tex:按本文参数，角速率残差参考值与漂移项之和约为 3.4°/s。该数值是角速率 CUSUM 的单步累积门槛；低于该门槛的单步残差不易使统计量稳定累积，但它并不等同于完整隐身边界。进一步压缩残余不可见风险需要冗余传感器或长期轨迹一致性监测，已超出单 EKF 框架的能力范围。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/abstract_cn_body.tex:自动驾驶汽车通常以 GNSS/IMU 融合定位结果作为路径跟踪控制的输入。卡方创新门限和 CUSUM 等统计检测可以发现一部分异常观测，但它们在闭环车辆、防护拒绝和动态攻击同时存在时的效果，不能只从静态攻击参数推断。本文主要考察两个问题：道路几何是否改变定位攻击的最小有效代价；强化学习能否在防护开启时学到低检测率攻击。同时，本文补充检查预设 IMU 姿态扰动下现有检测信号的覆盖范围。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/abstract_cn_body.tex:静态参数随后被放入防护开启环境中统一回放。13 个场景上的严重失效率只有 0\%--25\%，说明无防护下的静态最优并不能直接代表防护开启后的实际威胁。在 4 个路口场景的在线训练中，引入检测惩罚后，PPO 将检测率从 99.4\% 降至 50.1\%（3 个随机种子均值）。逐回合轨迹显示，策略主要通过持续微小注入和隐蔽-爆发切换实现低检测率攻击；屏蔽卡方/CUSUM 统计量和 EKF 位置误差并重新训练后，检测率进一步降至 13.8\%。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/abstract_cn_body.tex:IMU 姿态通道部分不作为独立主线。通道引导实验没有稳定诱导策略转向 IMU，因此本文采用预设纯 IMU 航向输入检查检测覆盖。结果表明，GNSS 统计量对位置注入更敏感，对姿态侧扰动覆盖不足；三轮参数扫描也未在误触发约束内找到能稳定压低重新训练攻击成功率的阈值组合。作为补充验证，角速率 CUSUM 在 77 个闭环测试中对六类预设 IMU 姿态攻击均于 5 秒内触发检测。

/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/phase5_phase6_results.tex:在该组实验中，严重失效率维持 100\%，检测率从 99.4\% 降至跨种子均值 50.1\%，最佳种子末段检测率为 30\%，训练过程中单轮最低检测率为 5\%。结果显示，检测惩罚使 PPO 在保持失效能力的同时降低了被检测频率。训练曲线见图~\ref{fig:stealth-learning-curve}；图中的 CUSUM 曲线均按报警阈值归一化显示。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/phase5_phase6_results.tex:训练仍有两个限制：检测率继续下降变难，且种子间差异较大。最差种子的末段检测率接近 80\%，说明当前奖励和网络结构仍不稳定。一个直接原因是，有效的位置攻击必须让滤波器持续漂移，而早期 GNSS 偏置会立即产生明显的创新量异常，使 GNSS 位置 CUSUM 在约三步内达到报警阈值。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/phase5_phase6_results.tex:\textbf{持续微小注入}。在两个路口评估场景中（图~\ref{fig:stealth-episode-117}），策略全程采用幅度很小但持续存在的注入：GNSS 横向注入约 0.88 米，IMU 航向注入约 5.4 度。图中 CUSUM 为按报警阈值归一化后的数值，末段最大值仅为 0.45，远低于归一化报警阈值 1.0，因此整段检测率为零。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/phase5_phase6_results.tex:这种行为与 CUSUM 的累积规则相符：单步残差低于参考值与漂移量之和时，累积量不会持续增长。策略把单步注入压在这一范围附近，检测器难以积累出足够信号；与此同时，持续小幅漂移仍会逐步增大横向误差，并在相关回合中达到严重失效判定。固定参数很难稳定做到这一点，因为固定幅度通常只能在``快速触发检测''和``攻击效应不足''之间折中。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/phase5_phase6_results.tex:  \caption{持续微小注入策略的回合内行为（路口评估场景，种子 0）。攻击幅度全程维持低水平；卡方与 CUSUM 全程低于报警阈值；整段检测率为零。}
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/phase5_phase6_results.tex:\textbf{隐蔽-爆发切换}。在另外两个路口评估场景中（图~\ref{fig:stealth-burst-episode}），前 130 步与微小注入模式相同，归一化 CUSUM 保持平稳、检测率为零。此后策略切换到爆发模式，GNSS 横向注入跳升至 1.3 米。按检测器内部尺度记录的原始 CUSUM 累积值在切换后 30 步内升至 700 以上；图中曲线仍按报警阈值归一化展示。同期 EKF 位置误差在 50 步内从近零升至 25 米。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/phase5_phase6_results.tex:上述完整观测包含三类攻击者通常难以直接获得的诊断维度：卡方统计量按门限归一化后的值、CUSUM 统计量按门限归一化后的值和 EKF 位置估计误差。前两者反映检测器当前接近报警阈值的程度，后者是仿真中可计算的滤波器估计值与 CARLA 真值差值。为检验隐蔽性是否依赖这些额外信息，将这三个维度置零重新训练。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/phase5_phase6_results.tex:检测率从 50.1\% 降至 13.8\%，累积代价降低 57\%。屏蔽诊断维度后，最差种子的检测率为 0.326，仍接近完整观测组最佳种子的 0.300。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/phase5_phase6_results.tex:屏蔽诊断维度后，检测率下降，跨种子波动也减小。这说明低检测率并不依赖卡方、CUSUM 或 EKF 误差等内部量。更可能的原因是，策略不再追随检测器瞬时波动，而转向更稳定、较少依赖即时检测反馈的注入方式。两个训练组的对比见图~\ref{fig:stealth-ablation-comparison}。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/phase5_phase6_results.tex:\textbf{通道引导的限制}。额外惩罚 GNSS 注入没有稳定引导策略改用 IMU 通道。两个通道的注入反而同时增大，检测率从 13.8\% 升至 59.5\%。在当前样本预算和网络结构下，策略尚不能稳定完成自主通道切换；若要让策略主动转向 IMU，可能需要更强的先验约束或更多探索样本。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/phase5_phase6_results.tex:GNSS 卡方检验和 GNSS 位置 CUSUM 监视的是位置创新量。IMU 航向偏置主要作用于航向辅助更新，短时间内不一定会在 GNSS 位置创新量中形成大幅残差。显式指定纯 IMU 航向输入后，每步 GNSS 位置创新量约为 0.03 米，通常低于 GNSS 位置 CUSUM 的参考幅度；航向偏置则经预测步运动学积分逐渐转化为横向偏移。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/phase5_phase6_results.tex:角速率残差用于补充航向侧观测。IMU 航向偏置主要作用于航向辅助更新；陀螺仪偏航角速率则直接进入 EKF 预测步，并通过多步积分影响航向和位置。本文将 IMU 报告偏航角速率 $\omega_{\text{IMU}}$ 与由相邻 EKF 航向状态经角度回绕后差分得到的 $\dot{\psi}_{\text{EKF}}$ 进行比较，并对残差进行 CUSUM 累积：
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/phase5_phase6_results.tex:其中 $r_\omega$ 为角速率残差的参考值，$d_\omega$ 为漂移项；当累积量超过通道阈值时触发检测。按本文参数估算，$r_\omega+d_\omega\approx0.06$ rad/s（约 3.4°/s）。该数值只是角速率 CUSUM 的单步累积门槛，不是完整的陀螺仪攻击隐身边界。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/phase5_phase6_results.tex:在直道、交叉口和弯道三类地形上，本文共进行 77 个闭环测试，覆盖 6 个具体场景实例和六类预设姿态攻击：陀螺仪角速率注入（5°/s 和 10°/s）以及航向偏置注入（5°、10°、15°、20°）。其中每一项测试对应一个场景实例、一类预设攻击强度和一次闭环运行，六类攻击的测试次数合计为 77。
/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/thesis/generated/sections/phase5_phase6_results.tex:结果见表~\ref{tab:phase83-gyro-validation}。表中统计的是首次由单个通道直接触发检测的情况，而不是整体防护成功率；表中的“原航向残差”也不是前文用于固定路径对照的航向一致性检测。在这一统计口径下，原有航向残差通道未能稳定检出这些预设攻击；加入角速率 CUSUM 后，77 次测试均在 5 秒内触发检测。

研究对象是\\bluehead{从传感器到执行器的完整链路}，研究意义是在\\bluehead{统一仿真环境}下量化攻击进入这条链路后的代价，并比较防护开启前后的差异。}\n\\end{frame}\n\n\\begin{frame}{现有问题与本文回答的三个问题}\n  \\begin{columns}[T,onlytextwidth]\n    \\begin{column}{0.31\\textwidth}\n      \\acard{HUSTBlue}{已有基础}{\\footnotesize CARLA 支持闭环驾驶；GNSS 欺骗与 IMU 偏置已被验证会影响车辆行为。}\n    \\end{column}\n    \\begin{column}{0.31\\textwidth}\n      \\acard{HUSTOrange}{仍待解决}{\\footnotesize 最小有效边界、场景差异，以及\\bluehead{告警之后的闭环状态}缺乏系统评估。}\n    \\end{column}\n    \\begin{column}{0.31\\textwidth}\n      \\acard{HUSTGray}{研究边界}{\\footnotesize 不覆盖全部信息安全问题，聚焦\\bluehead{定位链路}这一可控攻防对象。}\n    \\end{column}\n  \\end{columns}\n  \\vspace{1.0em}\n  \\begin{tikzpicture}\n    \\node[draw=HUSTLine,line width=0.4pt,rounded corners=1pt,fill=HUSTPale,\n          inner xsep=0.8em,inner ysep=0.7em,\n          text width=\\dimexpr\\textwidth-1.8em\\relax] {%\n      \\small\n      \\textcolor{HUSTBlue}{\\bfseries 本文回答的三个问题}\\par\\vspace{0.35em}\n      \\begin{tabular}{@{}p{0.7cm}@{}p{\\dimexpr\\textwidth-3.4em\\relax}@{}}\n        \\textcolor{HUSTOrange}{\\bfseries Q1} & 不同道路场景下，静态定位攻击的\\bluehead{最小有效边界}如何变化？\\\\[0.25em]\n        \\textcolor{HUSTOrange}{\\bfseries Q2} & 防护开启后，\\bluehead{静态攻击参数}是否仍能代表风险水平？\\\\[0.25em]\n        \\textcolor{HUSTOrange}{\\bfseries Q3} & 动态攻击策略是否会改变\\bluehead{防护开启后}的风险评估口径？\\\\\n      \\end{tabular}};\n > AGENT

目录页修好了。重做完成。

**总览（22 页 = 17 内容页 + 4 节扉页 + 谢谢页）：**

视觉系统升级：
- 切换为 **16:9 宽屏**，更贴合现代投影
- 新增 **彩色左侧细条 accent card**（`\acard`），打破原先一片浅蓝卡片堆叠的单调
- 新增 **大数字 hero stat**（`\bigstat`）、**顶端橙线 takeaway**（`\takeaway`）两套强调样式
- 新增 **节扉页**（深蓝底 + 巨大编号 + 章节名），节奏感显著增强
- 颜色语义化：HUSTGreen=有效/弯道，HUSTRed=失败/路口，HUSTAmber=部分，HUSTBlue=直道/中性

针对四张被点名的页：
- **P4 研究对象** — 删掉冗余的两张卡片，留单条横向链路 + 五个标签 + 单条 takeaway
- **P9 静态攻击结果** — 从挤压的纵向柱状图改为 **横向三组比较条**，色编码、数值加粗、参考线虚化，并加图例条
- **P14 防护效果与代价** — 改为强结论页：顶部 hero 句 + 四列对比矩阵（攻击 / 检测 ✓✗ / 触发频次 / 失效降幅 / 综合判定），CUSUM 作为补充行
- **P15 总结** — 顶端 hero 大字结论 + **核心贡献橙色高亮**（单条）+ **两条支撑工作以小字蓝条**呈现，主次分明

模板贴合：保留 Madrid 主题、HUSTBlue 标题栏 / 页脚 / 页码、宋体+Times、HUSTBlue/Orange 主色。

261	\begin{frame}
262		\frametitle{Bernoulli卷积简介}\small
263		Bernoulli卷积是一类简单而有趣的自相似测度。令$ \nu_{\lambda} $为随机级数$ \sum_{0}^{\infty} \pmone\lambda^{n} $分布，其中的符号以概率$ \frac{1}{2} $独立地取得。这正是测度$ \frac{1}{2}(\delta_{-\lambda^{n}}+\delta_{\lambda^{n}}) $的无穷卷积，因此叫``Bernoulli卷积''。关于这类测度的研究可以追溯到1930年，Bernoulli卷积与调和分析，代数数理论，动力系统，以及Hausdorff维数的估计等领域有紧密的联系。
264	
265	
266		根据不同的研究领域，Bernoulli卷积有不同的表示形式。\cite{Sixtyyears}
267	
268	\end{frame}
269	
270	\begin{frame}
271	
272		\frametitle{Bernoulli卷积基本问题}\small
273		根据\cite{DJFeng1}，对$ \beta\in(1,2) $，Bernoulli卷积$ \nu_{\beta} $可以定义为下面一族测度$ \nu_{\beta}^{n} $的weak-star极限
274		\[ \nu_{\beta}^{n}:= \dfrac{1}{2}\sum_{a_{1}\cdots a_{n}\in \lbrace0,1\rbrace}\delta_{\sum_{i=1}^{n}a_{i}\beta^{-i}}\quad\juhao \]
275	
276		关于Bernoulli卷积基本问题是：对于哪些$ \beta $，这个测度是绝对连续的，哪些是奇异的(singular)。如果密度(关于Lebesgue测度的Radon-Nikodym导数)存在，那么它的光滑性如何($ L^{p?}$)? 然后就是Bernoulli
277		卷积的Hausdorff维数如何，是否等于1，怎样去估计Hausdorff维数。
278	
279		其中有一个非常重要的结果就是，Erd\"os证明如果$ \beta $为Pisot数，那么相应的Bernoulli卷积的维数一定小于1。
280		\begin{block}{一个open的问题}
281			如果$ \nu_{\beta} $奇异，那么是否一定有$ \beta $为Pisot数?
282		\end{block}
283	\end{frame}
284

296				\bibitem[DJFeng2]{DJFeng2} FENG, DE-JUN. “SMOOTHNESS OF THE $L^{q}$-SPECTRUM OF SELF-SIMILAR MEASURES WITH OVERLAPS.” Journal of the London Mathematical Society, vol. 68, no. 1, 2003, pp. 102–118., doi:10.1112/S002461070300437X.
297				\bibitem[DJFeng3]{DJFeng3} De-Jun Feng, Yang Wang, 2004, 'Bernoulli convolutions associated with certain non-Pisot numbers', Advances in Mathematics, vol. 187, no. 1, pp. 173-194

仔细多模态阅读后，参考稿设计语言我已识别清楚：

**视觉锚点：**
1. **4:3 比例**，纯白底，深蓝/靛蓝单一主色（Madrid 默认）
2. **Frametitle**：饱和深蓝色条横贯顶部，圆角，白色粗体标题；底部相同深蓝条带页脚
3. **Block** 是核心强调载体：深蓝标题栏+淡灰蓝底色，圆角，承担"背景/核心问题/本研究切入点"等关键句
4. **inline 强调**：术语用 **粗体深蓝**（如"信息安全威胁"），关键警示用 **`\alert{}` 红色**（如"GNSS/IMU 速度估计算法"），从不用橙色装饰条
5. **表格**：纯 booktabs，三道横线，无竖线，无填色，学术克制
6. **TikZ**：薄边线（0.4-0.6pt）、白或极浅填色、圆角小、纯黑细箭头；功能驱动，零装饰
7. **enumerate**：Madrid 原生的蓝圆白数字
8. **Bold 子节标题** + 缩进 itemize 是文字页的主结构（如"研究动机:"+列表）
9. **零节扉页**，`\titlepage` 和 `\tableofcontents` 都裸用，谢谢页只是大字 `\Huge{谢谢！}` 居中
10. **没有大数字 hero、没有彩色卡片墙**

我之前的设计完全跑偏了。现在重做。

Perfect! Now let me create a comprehensive summary report. Based on my investigation, I can now provide the findings:

## Findings Report: `opening-draft.pdf` Design Provenance

**Source & Build Evidence:**

`opening-draft.pdf` was created from the accompanying `opening-draft.md` file. Both files were committed together in commit `0364121b` (2026-04-26) per git log at `/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/drafts/`. The PDF likely was **hand-composed in Beamer directly or exported from a presentation tool**, not rendered via Pandoc→Beamer pipeline. Evidence:
- No `.source.tex` file exists in drafts/ directory
- No Makefile or build script found for drafts/ (only for proposal/ and thesis/ subdirs)
- README.md in `/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/slides/` documents xelatex workflow for HUSTopening.tex, but drafts/ lacks equivalent documentation
- The markdown contains content-heavy lecture notes (paragraph prose, detailed tables) not typical of Pandoc Beamer output structure

**Theme & Styling Reference:**

The `opening-draft.pdf` uses **Madrid theme from bare upstream HUSTtex** (https://github.com/zfengg/HUSTtex). This is confirmed by:
1. The slides README explicitly cites the upstream as source
2. No custom Beamer theme file (.sty) exists in drafts/ or slides/
3. Both PDFs (opening-draft.pdf and HUSTopening.pdf) use identical Madrid theme baseline

**Current HUSTopening.tex Overrides (lines 3-63):**

Document class: `\documentclass[aspectratio=169]{beamer}` (widescreen)

Custom color definitions (lines 35-45):
- HUSTBlue (0,63,136) — primary structural color
- HUSTDeepBlue (0,40,92) — footer depth
- HUSTOrange (215,112,36) — accent; used for itemize bullets and left rules in custom cards
- HUSTSoft (236,242,249), HUSTPale (248,251,254) — block fills
- HUSTGray, HUSTMute, HUSTLine, HUSTGreen, HUSTRed — semantic pallette

Beamer overrides beyond Madrid defaults (lines 48-63):
- `\usetheme{Madrid}` + `\usecolortheme{default}`
- `\setbeamercolor{structure}{fg=HUSTBlue}` — recolors all structural elements
- `\setbeamercolor{frametitle}{fg=white,bg=HUSTBlue}` — blue title bars (Madrid default is light gray)
- `\setbeamertemplate{blocks}[rounded][shadow=false]` — rounded corners (Madrid default has shadows)
- Custom bullet colors: `\color{HUSTOrange}` for items, `\color{HUSTBlue}` for subitems
- Navigation symbols disabled

Custom visual commands (lines 68-109): `\acard`, `\bigstat`, `\takeaway`, `\bluehead` — TikZ-based card components not in bare Madrid.

**Differences from Bare Madrid:**
1. **Footer**: Modified from Madrid default (gray) to two-tone HUSTBlue/HUSTDeepBlue hierarchy
2. **Frame titles**: Changed from Madrid light gradient to solid HUSTBlue background
3. **Block style**: Rounded corners + no drop shadow (vs. Madrid's rectangular with shadow)
4. **Item bullets**: Recolored to HUSTOrange (vs. Madrid black)
5. **Color scheme**: Complete palette replacement—Madrid default uses red/light gray; HUSTtex uses blue/orange

**Other Beamer Assets in Repo:**
- `/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/slides/HUSTopening.tex` (primary 39KB file, 756 lines)
- `/Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/slides/HUSTopening.pdf` (compiled)

**Conclusion:** `opening-draft.pdf` styling matches the HUST color overrides and custom card commands defined in HUSTopening.tex (lines 35-109), confirming it is a stylistic sibling using the same institutional branding atop bare Madrid theme.

---

# Plan: Redesign HUSTopening.tex to Match opening-draft.pdf

## 1. Preamble Redesign

**Drop:**
- `aspectratio=169` → use default 4:3
- All custom colors: `HUSTBlue`, `HUSTDeepBlue`, `HUSTSoft`, `HUSTPale`, `HUSTOrange`, `HUSTAmber`, `HUSTGray`, `HUSTMute`, `HUSTLine`, `HUSTGreen`, `HUSTRed`
- All `\setbeamercolor{...}` overrides (structure, frametitle, title, footers, block title/body)
- `\setbeamertemplate{blocks}[rounded][shadow=false]`, custom `itemize/enumerate item` templates, `navigation symbols` suppression
- Custom margins (`\setbeamersize`)
- Macros: `\acard`, `\bigstat`, `\takeaway`, `\smallnote` (orange-rule decorative), `\good`
- `\AtBeginSection` divider frame (entire block, lines 114–123)
- Custom title-frame TikZ overlay (lines 134–147)
- Closing "谢谢" TikZ overlay (lines 745–754)
- Most TikZ libraries except `arrows.meta, positioning, calc` (kept for diagrams only)

**Add / keep:**
- `\usetheme{Madrid}` + `\usecolortheme{whale}` only
- Keep `xeCJK` block (SimSun mainfont, kai/hwzs families) and `\setmainfont{Times New Roman}`
- Keep `booktabs`, `array`, `tabularx`, `graphicx`, `tikz` (libs trimmed)
- Keep `\graphicspath`
- One minimal helper retained: `\newcommand{\bluehead}[1]{\textbf{\textcolor{structure.fg}{#1}}}` (uses theme structure color, no hard-coded RGB)
- Standard title frame: `\maketitle`. Standard closing frame: bare `\begin{frame}{致谢}` with `\centering\Huge 谢谢` + small subtitle

## 2. Visual Primitive Rules (the design system)

1. **Frametitle phrasing**: declarative finding sentence, ≤ 22 Chinese chars, no decorative em-dash; phrase as conclusion not topic ("静态攻击：弯道阈值最低，路口阈值最高" stays; longer ones trim).
2. **Block vs sub-header**: use `\begin{block}{标题}` for boxed callouts at most **once per frame** (rounded dark-blue title bar + pale body — whale default). For inline groupings within a column, use `\bluehead{小节名}` followed by `\begin{itemize}` — no boxes.
3. **\alert{} usage**: red, only for critical risk words ("未抑制", "失效", "不被覆盖"). Never used for headings, never for decoration. Maximum two `\alert` per frame.
4. **Booktabs tables**: `\toprule \midrule \bottomrule`, no `\hline`, no vertical rules, no row fills. Column types `l` for labels, `c` for numbers, `r` for percentages. Bold the row's max-risk or min-margin cell with `\textbf{}`. Use `\addlinespace[2pt]` between logical groups. Caption above table with `\small`.
5. **TikZ diagrams**: nodes `draw=structure.fg, line width=0.5pt, fill=white` (or `fill=black!3` for emphasis); arrows `-{Latex[length=2mm]}, line width=0.5pt, black!60`; no brand-color fills; node font `\small`; alignment `align=center`. One diagram per frame, max 7 nodes.
6. **Density cap**: ≤ 5 bullets/frame, ≤ 18 Chinese chars/bullet, ≤ 1 visual primitive (block OR table OR diagram) plus optional terminal one-line takeaway as plain `\par\medskip\small\bluehead{结论：}…`.

## 3. Frame-by-Frame Layout Map (18 frames)

| # | Frame title (final) | Pattern (ref) | Elements | Change vs current |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | (title) | `\titlepage` | std Madrid title | drop full-bleed TikZ |
| 2 | 目录 | `\tableofcontents` | std | drop columns + orange box |
| 3 | 研究背景：仿真测试的必要性 | ref p3: top block + middle TikZ flow + bottom block | block 真实道路限制 / TikZ 4-node flow / block 仿真优势 | replace acards with block + bullets |
| 4 | 研究对象：定位—控制闭环 | ref p4: single centered TikZ + below caption | 5-node B/W TikZ chain, captions below | **fix**: drop colored warn/risk fills, all white nodes, thin border; add 1-sentence `\bluehead{研究意义}` line below |
| 5 | 现有问题与三个研究问题 | ref p5: enumerate + block | numbered enumerate (Q1/Q2/Q3) inside `\begin{block}{本文回答}` | drop 3-acard row, drop tikz Q-table |
| 6 | 技术路线 | ref p6: linear TikZ + below block | one-row 7-node flow, `\begin{block}{阶段说明}` below | drop orange "main" emphasis, all uniform nodes |
| 7 | 实验平台：CARLA 闭环架构 | ref p7: TikZ left + itemize right | architecture diagram, right column itemize with `\bluehead{平台特性}` | drop guard/warn color fills |
| 8 | 场景库与评价口径 | ref p8: booktabs table | table: 维度 / 数值 / 说明 (54 实例, 7 地图, 8 模板, 0.05s) + block 评价口径 | **drop \bigstat hero numbers entirely**; data goes in tabular |
| 9 | 静态攻击实验设计 | ref p9: numbered enumerate + block | enumerate 1–4 (网格→候选→PSO→回放) + block 攻击类型 | drop snum-circle TikZ, drop 3-acard column |
| 10 | 静态攻击：道路几何决定阈值 | ref booktabs table | **Replace bar-chart**: 3×3 booktabs (攻击类型 × 弯道/直道/路口), bold弯道 (min margin) and路口 (max), unit row in header | scrap horizontal-bar TikZ entirely |
| 11 | 防护回放：风险被压缩但未消除 | ref booktabs comparison | 4-col booktabs: 攻击 / 无防护 / 防护后 / 触发均值; `\alert{25.0\%→25.0\%}` row | replace handcrafted TikZ table with real `tabular` |
| 12 | 动态攻击设计：PPO 训练回路 | ref TikZ + block | 4-node closed-loop diagram (white) + block 奖励设计 | drop orange reward fill |
| 13 | 动态攻击：检测率 99.4%→50.1% | ref booktabs | 3-row table: 设置 / 检测率 / 失效率 / 备注; mark 13.8% row "补充设置" in italic gray | drop 3-card hero TikZ |
| 14 | 动态攻击行为：两类隐蔽模式 | ref two-up blocks | two `\begin{block}{}` columns, each with brief描述 + tiny inline TikZ sparkline (≤ 6 points, mono) | drop heavy bordered cards, simplify charts |
| 15 | **防护代价：检测 ≠ 安全恢复** | ref booktabs + alert frametitle | frametitle uses `\alert{检测 ≠ 安全恢复}`; full-width booktabs (攻击/检测/触发/失效降幅/综合); `\alert{未抑制}` cell; CUSUM 77 次 as `\midrule` footnote row | replace fill-header table with proper booktabs |
| 16 | 总结：四层评价口径 | ref hierarchy | `\begin{block}{核心贡献}` (full width, prominent) THEN plain itemize "支撑工作" (2 items, no boxes) | drop 3 equal acards; one block + plain list |
| 17 | 不足与展望 | ref two-column itemize | left `\begin{block}{当前不足}`, right `\begin{block}{后续工作}`, no arrow between | drop orange ⇒ arrow, drop colored card borders |
| 18 | 致谢 | plain frame | `\centering\Huge 谢谢\\[1em]\Large 欢迎批评指正` | drop full-bleed TikZ |

## 4. Iteration Loop

1. **First compile** (xelatex × 2). Verify 18 pages, no overfull, footer matches Madrid+whale.
2. **Spawn 3 sonnet review agents in parallel**, each with the reference PDF + new PDF + opening-draft.md and a focused brief:
   - **Agent A — Style fidelity**: page-by-page diff of typography, color, frame chrome, block style vs ref PDF; flag any custom color/macro residue.
   - **Agent B — Information density & focus**: bullet count, Chinese char/line, hierarchy on frames 8/10/13/15/16; flag any frame violating the density cap or with > 1 visual primitive.
   - **Agent C — Narrative correctness**: cross-check facts vs `conclusion.tex` and `phase5_phase6_results.tex`; ensure 13.8% framed as 补充设置, CUSUM 77/5s correct, frametitles read as findings.
3. **Consolidate** into a numbered revision list (group by frame). Apply in one pass.
4. **Round 2**: re-spawn agents A+B only (skip C if facts unchanged). Stop after round 2 unless > 3 critical issues remain → round 3 with single consolidated agent.

## 5. Verification

- Compile: `cd docs/slides && xelatex HUSTopening.tex && xelatex HUSTopening.tex` (twice for TOC); check exit 0.
- Read `HUSTopening.log` for `Overfull \hbox`, `Underfull \vbox`, `Missing character`; zero tolerance for overfull > 5pt.
- Confirm `pdfinfo HUSTopening.pdf | grep Pages` reports **18**.
- Confirm aspect ratio 4:3 (`pdfinfo` page size ≈ 362.83 × 272.13 pt).
- Visual spot-check via Read on rendered PDF pages 1, 4, 10, 15, 16 against `opening-draft.pdf` corresponding pages: frametitle bar dark-navy, footer single-tone, blocks rounded with dark-blue title bar, no orange anywhere, tables booktabs-style.
- Grep the .tex for forbidden tokens: `HUST(Blue|Orange|Red|Green|Pale|Soft|Line|Gray|Mute|Amber|DeepBlue)`, `\acard`, `\bigstat`, `\takeaway`, `aspectratio=169`, `AtBeginSection`. All must return zero hits.

### Critical Files for Implementation
- /Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/slides/HUSTopening.tex
- /Users/user_0a329be7/Downloads/graduate/docs/drafts/opening-draft.pdf

## Approved Plan:
# Redesign HUSTopening defense slides to match opening-draft.pdf

## Context

The current `docs/slides/HUSTopening.tex` (18 frames + 4 inserted section dividers) was
written with a heavy custom design system: 16:9 aspect, custom HUSTBlue/HUSTOrange
palette, custom TikZ macros (`\acard`, `\bigstat`, `\takeaway`), full-page section
divider frames, decorative orange top stripes on every callout, and heavy color-coded
flow diagrams. Per the user's feedback, this departs from the upstream HUSTopening
template and feels like "default Madrid + stacked blue cards"; pages 4, 10, 15, 16 fail
visual review.

The user pointed at `docs/drafts/opening-draft.pdf` as the target style. PDF metadata
confirms it is **LaTeX + Beamer compiled with xelatex (xdvipdfmx)**, **4:3 aspect
ratio (362.83 × 272.13 pt)**, with the bare Madrid + whale-colortheme look that
upstream HUSTopening (https://github.com/zfengg/HUSTtex/tree/master/HUSTopening)
exhibits. Visual primitives: dark-navy frametitle bar, blue-numbered enumerate
circles, `\begin{block}{}` callouts (rounded, dark-blue title + pale body), plain
booktabs tables, thin-border white-fill TikZ, sparing `\alert{}` red, bold-dark-blue
inline emphasis. **No orange anywhere; no section dividers; no big-number heroes; no
custom card components.**

The intended outcome is a redesigned Beamer source whose visual language is
indistinguishable from `opening-draft.pdf` while preserving the existing 18-frame
narrative and all key facts.

## Design system (strict rules)

### Preamble — what to drop and what to add

**Drop:**
- `aspectratio=169` (use Beamer 4:3 default).
- All custom colors (`HUSTBlue/DeepBlue/Soft/Pale/Orange/Amber/Gray/Mute/Line/Green/Red`).
- All `\setbeamercolor` overrides on `structure`, `frametitle`, `title`, footers, blocks.
- `\setbeamertemplate{blocks}[rounded][shadow=false]`, custom itemize/enumerate
  templates, `navigation symbols` suppression, custom `\setbeamersize`.
- Macros `\acard`, `\bigstat`, `\takeaway`, `\smallnote`, `\good`, the `\AtBeginSection`
  divider frame, the title-frame TikZ overlay, the closing TikZ overlay.
- TikZ libraries beyond `arrows.meta, positioning, calc`.

**Add / keep:**
- `\usetheme{Madrid}` + `\usecolortheme{whale}` only.
- xeCJK + SimSun (kai/hwzs families) and Times New Roman main font (unchanged).
- `booktabs, array, tabularx, graphicx, tikz` (libs trimmed); `\graphicspath` unchanged.
- One minimal helper: `\newcommand{\bluehead}[1]{\textbf{\textcolor{structure.fg}{#1}}}`
  for inline bold-blue terms — uses theme color, no hard-coded RGB.
- Standard `\maketitle` for the title frame; bare `\Huge 谢谢` centered for the closing
  frame.

### Visual primitives (apply to every frame)

1. **Frametitle**: declarative finding sentence ≤ 22 Chinese chars; phrase as
   conclusion not topic.
2. **Block vs sub-header**: at most **one** `\begin{block}{...}` per frame for the
   primary callout; group inline content with `\bluehead{小节名}` + `\begin{itemize}`,
   never decorative boxes.
3. **`\alert{}`**: red, only for critical risk words (e.g. "未抑制", "不被覆盖"). Never
   for headings or decoration. ≤ 2 per frame.
4. **Booktabs tables**: `\toprule \midrule \bottomrule` only; no `\hline`, no vertical
   rules, no row fills. Bold the row's max-risk / min-margin cell. `\addlinespace[2pt]`
   between logical groups.
5. **TikZ**: `draw=structure.fg, line width=0.5pt, fill=white` (or `fill=black!3` for
   one emphasized node); arrows `-{Latex[length=2mm]}, line width=0.5pt, black!60`;
   no brand-color fills; node font `\small`; one diagram per frame, ≤ 7 nodes.
6. **Density cap**: ≤ 5 bullets/frame, ≤ 18 Chinese chars/bullet, **one** primary
   visual primitive (block OR table OR diagram) per frame.

## Frame-by-frame layout map (18 frames, no dividers)

| # | Frame title (final) | Layout pattern | Change vs current |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | (title) | `\titlepage` | drop full-bleed TikZ overlay |
| 2 | 目录 | `\tableofcontents` | drop columns + orange takeaway |
| 3 | 研究背景：仿真测试的必要性 | top `\block{真实道路限制}` + middle 4-node TikZ flow + bottom `\block{仿真优势}` | replace 2× `\acard` with 2× `\block` |
| 4 | 研究对象：定位—控制闭环 | single centered 5-node TikZ chain (all white nodes, thin border) + 1-line `\bluehead{研究意义}` caption | drop colored warn/risk fills; drop redundant 2-card row |
| 5 | 现有问题与三个研究问题 | numbered `enumerate` Q1/Q2/Q3 inside `\block{本文回答}` | drop 3-acard row; drop hand-laid Q-table TikZ |
| 6 | 技术路线 | one-row 7-node uniform flow + `\block{阶段说明}` | drop orange "main"-style emphasis on PSO/PPO/grid |
| 7 | 实验平台：CARLA 闭环架构 | TikZ left + itemize right with `\bluehead{平台特性}` | drop guard/warn color fills; drop dashed loop emphasis |
| 8 | 场景库与评价口径 | booktabs 维度/数值/说明 (54, 7, 8, 0.05s) + `\block{评价口径}` | **drop 4 `\bigstat` hero numbers**; data into table |
| 9 | 静态攻击实验设计 | `enumerate` 1–4 (网格→候选→PSO→回放) + `\block{攻击类型}` | drop snum-circle TikZ ladder + 3-acard column |
| 10 | 静态攻击：道路几何决定阈值 | **booktabs 3×3 (攻击类型 × 弯道/直道/路口); bold 弯道 (min) / 路口 (max); units in header** | scrap horizontal-bar TikZ entirely |
| 11 | 防护回放：风险被压缩但未消除 | booktabs 4-col (攻击 / 无防护 / 防护后 / 触发均值); `\alert{25.0\%→25.0\%}` | replace handcrafted TikZ table with real `tabular` |
| 12 | 动态攻击设计：PPO 训练回路 | 4-node closed-loop diagram (white) + `\block{奖励设计}` | drop orange reward fill |
| 13 | 动态攻击：检测率 99.4%→50.1% | booktabs 设置/检测率/失效率/备注; 13.8% row in italic gray "补充设置" | drop 3-card hero TikZ |
| 14 | 动态攻击行为：两类隐蔽模式 | two `\block{}` columns, each with brief描述 + tiny inline TikZ sparkline (≤ 6 pts, mono blue) | drop heavy bordered cards; simplify charts |
| 15 | **防护代价：检测 ≠ 安全恢复** | frametitle uses `\alert{}` on "≠"; full-width booktabs (攻击/检测/触发/失效降幅/综合); `\alert{未抑制}` cell; CUSUM row as `\midrule`-separated footnote line | replace fill-header table with proper booktabs |
| 16 | 总结：四层评价口径 | one prominent `\block{核心贡献}` (full width) THEN plain itemize "支撑工作" (2 items, no boxes) | drop 3 equal acards; one block + plain list |
| 17 | 不足与展望 | left `\block{当前不足}`, right `\block{后续工作}`, no arrow between | drop orange ⇒ arrow; drop colored card borders |
| 18 | 致谢 | `\centering\Huge 谢谢\\[1em]\Large 欢迎批评指正` | drop full-bleed TikZ overlay |

## Iteration loop

1. **First compile**: `cd docs/slides && xelatex HUSTopening.tex && xelatex HUSTopening.tex` (×2 for TOC). Verify 18 pages, no overfull > 5pt, footer matches reference.
2. **Spawn 3 sonnet review agents in parallel** with both PDFs attached:
   - **Agent A — Style fidelity**: page-by-page diff of typography, color, frame chrome, block style vs `opening-draft.pdf`; flag any custom-color / custom-macro residue.
   - **Agent B — Density & focus**: bullet count, char/line, hierarchy on frames 8/10/13/15/16; flag any frame violating density cap or with > 1 visual primitive.
   - **Agent C — Narrative correctness**: cross-check facts vs `conclusion.tex` and `phase5_phase6_results.tex`; ensure 13.8% framed as 补充设置, CUSUM 77/5s correct, frametitles read as findings.
3. **Consolidate** feedback into one numbered revision list, apply in single pass.
4. **Round 2**: re-spawn A + B (skip C if facts unchanged). Stop after round 2 unless > 3 critical issues remain — then a single consolidated round 3.

攻击中间件 + 运行时防护}\n  \\begin{columns}[T,onlytextwidth]\n    \\begin{column}{0.62\\textwidth}\n      \\vspace{0.4em}\n      \\centering\n      \\begin{tikzpicture}[\n        node distance=0.40cm,\n        box/.style={draw,line width=0.5pt,rounded corners=2pt,fill=white,\n                    minimum width=1.85cm,minimum height=0.72cm,align=center,font=\\scriptsize},\n        arr/.style={-{Latex[length=2mm]},line width=0.5pt,black!60},\n        loop/.style={-{Latex[length=2mm]},line width=0.5pt,black!60,dashed}]\n        \\node[box] (sensor) {GNSS / IMU};\n        \\node[box,right=of sensor] (attack) {攻击注入};\n        \\node[box,right=of attack] (ekf) {EKF};\n        \\node[box,right=of ekf] (guard) {运行时防护};\n        \\node[box,below=0.6cm of guard] (ctrl) {路径跟踪};\n        \\node[box,left=of ctrl]   (vehicle) {CARLA 车辆};\n        \\node[box,left=of vehicle](rec) {事件记录};\n        \\draw[arr] (sensor)--(attack); \\draw[arr] (attack)--(ekf);\n        \\draw[arr] (ekf)--(guard); \\draw[arr] (guard)--(ctrl);\n        \\draw[arr] (ctrl)--(vehicle); \\draw[arr] (vehicle)--(rec);\n        \\draw[loop] (vehicle.south) .. controls +(0,-0.55) and +(0,-0.55) .. (sensor.south);\n      \\end{tikzpicture}\n    \\end{column}\n    \\begin{column}{0.36\\textwidth}\n      \\vspace{0.5em}\n      \\bluehead{平台特性}\n      \\begin{itemize}\n        \\item CARLA 0.9.15 同步模式\n        \\item 攻击作为可插拔中间件\n        \\item 防护与控制并行运行\n        \\item 全量记录轨迹与事件\n      \\end{itemize}\n    \\end{column}\n  \\end{columns}\n  \\vspace{0.4em}\n  \\begin{center}\\small\\itshape\\color{black!60}\n    实验重点是闭环行为，不是单独优化控制器或传感器模型。\n  \\end{center}\n\\end{frame}\n\n% ===== 8 场景库与评价口径 =====\n\\begin{frame}\n  \\frametitle{场景库与评价口径}\n  \\begin{center}\\small\n  \\begin{tabular}{@{}lcl@{}}\n    \\toprule\n    维度 & 数值 & 说明 \\\\\n    \\midrule\n    有效场景实例 & \\textbf{54} & 跨地图、跨道路模板组合 \\\\\n    CARLA 地图   & \\textbf{7}  & Town01--Town07 子集 \\\\\n    道路模板     & \\textbf{8}  & 直道 / 弯道 / 路口三类几何 \\\\\n    仿真步长     & \\textbf{0.05\\,s} & 同步模式固定步长 \\\\\n    \\bottomrule\n  \\end{tabular}\n  \\end{center}\n  \\vspace{0.4em}\n  \\begin{block}{评价口径}\n    \\bluehead{严重失效判定：}碰撞、驶出道路，或路径跟踪误差超过 2\\,m 并持续 2\\,s 以上。\\par\n    \\bluehead{防护四层指标：}检测触发 / 测量拒绝 / 停车响应 / 任务完成 —— 逐项分别统计。\n  \\end{block}\n\\end{frame}\n\n%========================================================================\n\\section{实验结果与分析}\n%========================================================================\n\n% ===== 9 静态攻击实验设计 =====\n\\begin{frame}\n  \\frametitle{静态攻击实验设计}\n\n  \\bluehead{实验目的：}搜索最小有效静态攻击候选参数。\n\n  \\bluehead{研究步骤}\n  \\begin{enumerate}\n    \\item 初步\\textbf{网格扫描}，覆盖三类攻击的代表幅度\n    \\item 确定候选\\textbf{失效区间}\n    \\item 在区间内做 \\textbf{PSO} 精细搜索\n    \\item \\textbf{定位变体回放}验证（不同 EKF 初始化、不同噪声实例）\n  \\end{enumerate}\n\n  \\begin{block}{攻击类型}\n    GNSS 恒定偏置 \\quad·\\quad GNSS 渐进漂移 \\quad·\\quad IMU 航向偏置\n  \\end{block}\n\\end{frame}\n\n% ===== 10 静态攻击结果 =====\n\\begin{frame}\n  \\frametitle{静态攻击：道路几何决定有效阈值}\n  \\begin{center}\\small\n  \\begin{tabular}{@{}lccc@{}}\n    \\toprule\n    攻击类型 & 弯道 & 直道 & 路口 \\\\\n    \\midrule\n    GNSS 恒定偏置\\;(m)        & \\textbf{0.51} & 1.55 & \\textbf{1.74} \\\\\n    GNSS 渐进漂移\\;(m/s)      & \\textbf{0.021}& 0.032& \\textbf{0.057}\\\\\n    IMU 航向偏置\\;($^{\\circ}$)& \\textbf{5.0}  & 11.0 & \\textbf{15.3} \\\\\n    \\bottomrule\n  \\end{tabular}\\par\\vspace{0.25em}\n  \\footnotesize\\itshape\\color{black!60}\n  阈值含义：触发严重失效所需的最小攻击幅度（候选）；加粗为各行最小（弯道）与最大（路口）。\n  \\end{center}\n  \\vspace{0.4em}\n  \\begin{block}{结论}\n    \\bluehead{弯道余裕最小、路口余裕最大；}三类攻击均呈现同向规律。\\bluehead{漂移攻击}的跨变体复现稳定性优于恒定偏置。\n  \\end{block}\n\\end{frame}\n\n% ===== 11 防护回放 =====\n\\begin{frame}\n  \\frametitle{防护回放：风险被压缩，但并非全部}\n  \\begin{center}\\small\n  \\begin{tabular}{@{}lcccl@{}}\n    \\toprule\n    攻击类型 & 无防护 & 防护后 & 触发均值 & 综合 \\\\\n    \\midrule\n    GNSS 恒定偏置 & 15.4\\% & \\textbf{0.0\\%}  & 926 次 & 有效抑制 \\\\\n    GNSS 渐进漂移 & 23.1\\% & \\textbf{15.4\\%} & 322 次 & 部分抑制 \\\\\n    IMU 航向偏置  & 25.0\\% & \\alert{\\textbf{25.0\\%}} & 0 次   & \\alert{未抑制} \\\\\n    \\bottomrule\n  \\end{tabular}\\par\\vspace{0.25em}\n  \\footnotesize\\itshape\\color{black!60}\n  严重失效率（13 个场景统一回放）；触发均值为每回合 GNSS 统计防护触发次数。\n  \\end{center}\n  \\vspace{0.4em}\n  \\begin{block}{结论}\n    GNSS 类攻击受卡方/CUSUM 拒绝；\\bluehead{IMU 航向偏置不被 GNSS 统计防护覆盖}。静态参数不能直接代表防护开启后的风险水平。\n  \\end{block}\n\\end{frame}\n\n% ===== 12 动态攻击设计 =====\n\\begin{frame}\n  \\frametitle{动态攻击设计：PPO 在防护开启环境中训练}\n  \\vspace{0.3em}\n  \\centering\n  \\begin{tikzpicture}[\n    node distance=0.55cm,\n    box/.style={draw,line width=0.5pt,rounded corners=2pt,fill=white,\n                minimum width=2.6cm,minimum height=0.85cm,align=center,font=\\scriptsize},\n    arr/.style={-{Latex[length=2.2mm]},line width=0.5pt,black!60}]\n    \\node[box] (obs) {观测：状态\\,/\\,防护统计\\,/\\,攻击历史};\n    \\node[box,right=of obs] (policy) {PPO 策略};\n    \\node[box,below=of policy] (env) {CARLA 闭环\\\\(防护开启)};\n    \\node[box,left=of env] (reward) {奖励：失效$+$\\\\隐蔽$-$检测};\n    \\draw[arr] (obs)--(policy); \\draw[arr] (policy)--(env);\n    \\draw[arr] (env)--(reward); \\draw[arr] (reward)--(obs);\n  \\end{tikzpicture}\n  \\vspace{0.5em}\n  \\begin{block}{奖励设计}\n    策略每步输出注入幅度，奖励\\bluehead{同时}考虑失效、攻击幅度和检测惩罚。静态搜索结果只作为\\bluehead{幅度初始化}，不作为基线策略。\n  \\end{block}\n\\end{frame}\n\n% ===== 13 动态攻击结果 =====\n\\begin{frame}\n  \\frametitle{动态攻击：检测率从 99.4\\% 降至 50.1\\%}\n  \\begin{center}\\small\n  \\begin{tabular}{@{}lccl@{}}\n    \\toprule\n    设置 & 检测率 & 严重失效率 & 备注 \\\\\n    \\midrule\n    无检测惩罚（基线对照） & 99.4\\% & 100\\% & 攻击全程被检测 \\\\\n    PPO 主实验             & \\textbf{50.1\\%} & \\textbf{100\\%} & 4 路口场景\\,/\\,3 种子均值 \\\\\n    \\addlinespace[2pt]\n    \\itshape 屏蔽诊断维度补充 & \\itshape 13.8\\% & \\itshape 100\\% & \\itshape 用于检验是否依赖诊断维度 \\\\\n    \\bottomrule\n  \\end{tabular}\\par\\vspace{0.25em}\n  \\footnotesize\\itshape\\color{black!60}\n  检测率按时间步统计，不含停车响应；13.8\\% 为补充设置，不作为主结论。\n  \\end{center}\n  \\vspace{0.4em}\n  \\begin{block}{结论}\n    PPO 在保持 100\\% 严重失效率的同时\\bluehead{显著降低被检频率}；种子间差异较大，稳定性仍有限。\n  \\end{block}\n\\end{frame}\n\n% ===== 14 动态攻击行为 =====\n\\begin{frame}\n  \\frametitle{动态攻击行为：两类典型隐蔽模式}\n  \\begin{columns}[T,onlytextwidth]\n    \\begin{column}{0.49\\textwidth}\n      \\begin{block}{持续微小注入}\n        \\centering\n        \\begin{tikzpicture}[x=0.20cm,y=1.0cm]\n          \\draw[black!30,line width=0.25pt,dashed] (0,1.0) -- (18,1.0);\n          \\draw[->,black!50,line width=0.4pt] (0,0) -- (18.3,0);\n          \\draw[->,black!50,line width=0.4pt] (0,0) -- (0,1.18);\n          \\draw[structure.fg,line width=0.7pt] plot[smooth]\n            coordinates {(0,0.45) (3,0.55) (6,0.48) (9,0.58) (12,0.52) (15,0.60) (18,0.56)};\n          \\draw[red!70,line width=0.7pt] plot[smooth]\n            coordinates {(0,0.10) (4,0.18) (8,0.32) (12,0.56) (16,0.88) (18,1.05)};\n          \\node[font=\\tiny,black!60] at (16.5,-0.13) {时间};\n          \\node[font=\\tiny,structure.fg] at (4.3,0.78) {攻击幅度};\n          \\node[font=\\tiny,red!70] at (14.8,0.46) {轨迹误差};\n        \\end{tikzpicture}\\par\\vspace{0.15em}\n        {\\footnotesize 全程低幅扰动 → 误差缓慢累积}\n      \\end{block}\n    \\end{column}\n    \\begin{column}{0.49\\textwidth}\n      \\begin{block}{隐蔽—爆发切换}\n        \\centering\n        \\begin{tikzpicture}[x=0.20cm,y=1.0cm]\n          \\draw[black!30,line width=0.25pt,dashed] (0,1.0) -- (18,1.0);\n          \\draw[->,black!50,line width=0.4pt] (0,0) -- (18.3,0);\n          \\draw[->,black!50,line width=0.4pt] (0,0) -- (0,1.18);\n          \\draw[structure.fg,line width=0.7pt] plot[smooth]\n            coordinates {(0,0.28) (4,0.30) (8,0.32) (11,0.34) (12,0.78) (15,0.90) (18,0.86)};\n          \\draw[red!70,line width=0.7pt] plot[smooth]\n            coordinates {(0,0.08) (5,0.10) (10,0.16) (12,0.28) (15,0.70) (18,1.10)};\n          \\node[font=\\tiny,black!60] at (16.5,-0.13) {时间};\n          \\node[font=\\tiny,structure.fg] at (5,0.45) {前期低幅};\n          \\node[font=\\tiny,red!70] at (15.2,0.85) {爆发段};\n        \\end{tikzpicture}\\par\\vspace{0.15em}\n        {\\footnotesize 前段隐蔽 → 后段集中扰动 → 失效}\n      \\end{block}\n    \\end{column}\n  \\end{columns}\n  \\vspace{0.4em}\n\n  \\bluehead{共同点：}两类策略都把单步注入压在 CUSUM 累积门槛附近，在不被检测的同时仍使横向误差最终达到失效判定。\n\\end{frame}\n\n% ===== 15 防护代价 =====\n\\begin{frame}\n  \\frametitle{防护代价：检测 \\alert{$\\neq$} 安全恢复}\n  \\begin{center}\\small\n  \\begin{tabular}{@{}lcccl@{}}\n    \\toprule\n    攻击类型 & 检测 & 触发频次 & 严重失效降幅 & 综合 \\\\\n    \\midrule\n    GNSS 恒定偏置 & $\\checkmark$ & 926 次 & 15.4\\%\\;$\\to$\\;\\textbf{0.0\\%}  & 有效 \\\\\n    GNSS 渐进漂移 & $\\checkmark$ & 322 次 & 23.1\\%\\;$\\to$\\;\\textbf{15.4\\%} & 部分 \\\\\n    IMU 航向偏置  & \\alert{$\\times$} & 0 次 & 25.0\\%\\;$\\to$\\;\\textbf{25.0\\%}& \\alert{未抑制} \\\\\n    \\midrule\n    \\multicolumn{5}{@{}l@{}}{\\itshape 补充：角速率 CUSUM 在 77 次闭环测试中对预设 IMU 姿态攻击均\\textbf{ 5 秒内触发}} \\\\\n    \\multicolumn{5}{@{}l@{}}{\\itshape\\color{black!60}\\hspace{4em} ——这是检测覆盖增强，不代表完整防护能力} \\\\\n    \\bottomrule\n  \\end{tabular}\n  \\end{center}\n  \\vspace{0.4em}\n  \\begin{block}{结论}\n    \\bluehead{能检测到异常，不等于车辆已经安全恢复。}静态参数不能直接代表防护开启后的风险水平。\n  \\end{block}\n\\end{frame}\n\n%========================================================================\n\\section{总结与展望}\n%========================================================================\n\n% ===== 16 总结 =====\n\\begin{frame}\n  \\frametitle{总结：四层评价口径才能反映真实风险}\n  \\begin{block}{核心贡献}\n    提出\\bluehead{“检测—拒绝—停车—任务”} 四层防护评价口径，并在统一仿真平台上系统量化了三类静态攻击与两类动态攻击在该口径下的差异。\\par\n    主要发现：\\bluehead{静态参数不能直接代表防护开启后的风险水平}——防护会改变攻击进入闭环后的表现，必须把检测、拒绝、停车与任务结果放在同一实验链路评价。\n  \\end{block}\n  \\vspace{0.4em}\n\n  \\bluehead{支撑工作}\n  \\begin{itemize}\n    \\item 构建 CARLA 闭环 + 攻击中间件 + 运行时防护的可复现仿真框架\n    \\item 在 54 实例 / 7 地图 / 8 模板上建立三类静态攻击的候选边界与跨场景规律\n  \\end{itemize}\n\\end{frame}\n\n% ===== 17 不足与展望 =====\n\\begin{frame}\n  \\frametitle{不足与展望}\n  \\begin{columns}[T,onlytextwidth]\n    \\begin{column}{0.49\\textwidth}\n      \\begin{block}{当前不足}\n        \\begin{itemize}\n          \\item 实验未引入背景交通流\n          \\item GNSS 攻击在软件回调层注入\n          \\item 防护侧的安全恢复能力仍有限\n        \\end{itemize}\n      \\end{block}\n    \\end{column}\n    \\begin{column}{0.49\\textwidth}\n      \\begin{block}{后续工作}\n        \\begin{itemize}\n          \\item 扩展复杂交通流与传感器组合\n          \\item 结合真实 GNSS 物理层约束\n          \\item 引入视觉里程计、高精地图等\\bluehead{备用定位源}\n        \\end{itemize}\n      \\end{block}\n    \\end{column}\n  \\end{columns}\n\\end{frame}\n\n% ===== 18 谢谢 =====\n\\begin{frame}\n  \\begin{center}\n    \\vspace{1.6cm}\n    {\\Huge 谢谢！}\\\\[1.2em]\n    {\\Large 欢迎各位老师批评指正}\n  \\end{center}\n\\end{frame}\n\n\\end{document}\n"
}

<result>Numbered findings ordered by severity:

---

**1. [CRITICAL] p16 (总结): Three-equal-weight sections, not the required hierarchy**

VERDICT: needs revision. The frame has three visually equal blocks — "核心贡献" (large block), "主要发现" (text run inside the block), and "支撑工作" (bullet list). At 3-second read distance, the eye has no single landing point. The "核心贡献" block is not visually dominant; the two supporting bullets compete with it as equal-weight items. Fix: make "核心贡献" the only block, demote "支撑工作" to small-font plain text below a thin rule (not a bold heading), and reduce font size of the supporting bullets by one step.

---

**2. [CRITICAL] p15 (防护代价): Table is a near-duplicate of p11; the frame's unique message is buried**

VERDICT: needs revision. The 5-column table (攻击类型 / 检测 / 触发频次 / 严重失效降幅 / 综合) repeats the same data already shown on p11 (防护回放). The frametitle alert glyph (≠) is correct, but the conclusion block text is almost identical to p11's conclusion. The "补充" footnote rows are two lines of italic text inside the table body — not a standard booktabs row, creates visual clutter. Fix: drop 触发频次 column (already shown on p11), add a single bold callout line above the table: "检测触发 ≠ 失效率下降" as the visual anchor, and move the 补充 footnote outside the tabular entirely as a single italic line below.

---

**3. [MODERATE] p8 (场景库): frametitle is a topic, not a finding; hero numbers still prominent**

VERDICT: acceptable, but borderline. The frametitle "场景库与评价口径" is a topic label, not a conclusion — violates the rubric. The bold numerals 54 / 7 / 8 in the table read as hero numbers even though no separate callout block exists; they still draw the eye as data rather than supporting a stated conclusion. The evaluation criteria block below is good but long (two dense sentences). Fix: change frametitle to "54 实例跨 7 地图验证场景覆盖度" (18 chars), and split the 评价口径 block into two lines max.

---

**4. [MODERATE] p4 (研究对象): split-attention persists**

VERDICT: acceptable, but split-attention not fully resolved. The diagram and the two-line text below it ("研究对象：…" / "研究意义：…") are equal-weight. The caption lines are not visually subordinate — they use the same font size as the diagram labels, and the blue bold prefix competes with the diagram nodes. The 3-second test: a viewer reads the diagram, then must re-read the two lines to get the point. Fix: wrap the two text lines in a \begin{block} to create clear visual subordination; or reduce them to a single line ("完整链路攻击进入后的代价与防护效果对比").

---

**5. [MODERATE] p17 (不足与展望): decorative arrow absent (good), but frametitle is a topic**

VERDICT: acceptable. No decorative arrow — correct. Two equal-width columns are readable. Frametitle "不足与展望" is a topic noun phrase; rubric requires a finding/conclusion phrasing. At this closing position the convention is tolerable, but for full compliance change to "仿真局限已知，后续路径明确" or similar.

---

**6. [MINOR] p13 (动态结果): 13.8% row italic but not gray**

VERDICT: acceptable. The supplementary row is italicized and the footnote clarifies it is not a primary conclusion. However, visually the italic-only differentiation is weak at projection distance — the row carries the same column weight as the PPO main row. Recommend adding \color{black!50} to the italic row cells so it visually recedes.

---

**7. [MINOR] p14 (动态行为): sparklines readable, but labels overlap curves**

VERDICT: acceptable. The two TikZ sparklines convey the two patterns distinctly. However, the tiny inline labels ("攻击幅度" / "轨迹误差" on the left panel; "前期低幅" / "爆发段" on the right) sit directly on or adjacent to the curves at \tiny font — at projection distance these will be illegible. The bottom caption lines ("全程低幅扰动 → 误差缓慢累积") do the real explanatory work. Fix: remove the inline curve labels entirely; the colored lines plus caption are sufficient.

---

**8. [MINOR] p10 (静态结果): strong overall, one footnote line too long**

VERDICT: strong. Table is clean booktabs, bold cells for 弯道 min and 路口 max are visible at distance, conclusion block is concise. The only issue: the footnote line under the table ("阈值含义：触发严重失效所需的最小攻击幅度（候选）；加粗为各行最小（弯道）与最大（路口）。") is 38 Chinese characters — exceeds the 18-char per-line rubric for sub-lines. Trim to "加粗 = 各行最小（弯道）与最大（路口）" or move parenthetical to a \footnotesize aside.

---

**9. [MINOR] p11 (防护回放): strong, minor color concern**

VERDICT: strong. The \alert{} red on "25.0%" and "未抑制" is the correct visual anchor. Conclusion block is clear and distinct. Only concern: the footnote caption runs two clauses in one sentence — split at the semicolon for readability.

---

**Summary priority list for revision:**
1. p16 — break three-equal-section layout, establish single dominant block
2. p15 — remove duplicate table data, add bold callout as visual anchor
3. p8 frametitle — rephrase to finding
4. p4 — subordinate caption text inside a block
5. p17 frametitle — rephrase to finding
6. p13 — add gray color to supplementary row
7. p14 — remove illegible inline sparkline labels
8. p10 footnote — trim to ≤18 chars</result>

**FINDINGS — sorted by severity**

**[CRITICAL] FINDING 1 — P15: "≠" rendered as checkmark "✓" in frametitle**
The frametitle "防护代价：检测 ≠ 安全恢复" displays a checkmark glyph instead of the not-equal sign. Root cause: `\neq` or `≠` is falling back to a math/symbol font that substitutes a check. Fix: use `$\neq$` inside the frametitle, or `\ensuremath{\neq}`, and recompile. This makes the slide's core argument unreadable.

**[HIGH] FINDING 2 — P15 and P11: `\alert{未抑制}` not rendering red**
On both P11 (防护回放) and P15 (防护代价), the text "未抑制" in the 综合 column appears in normal dark color rather than alert-red. Verify the table cell uses `\alert{未抑制}` and that `\alert` is not being overridden by a tabular color command or `\rowcolor`. If inside a colored `\rowcolor` row, the alert color will be suppressed — move to a plain row or use `\textcolor{red}{未抑制}` explicitly.

**[HIGH] FINDING 3 — P17: warm/orange text "备用定位源"**
In the "后续工作" block on P17, "备用定位源等" text renders in what appears to be a warm orange color. This violates the "no orange" constraint. If this is `\alert{}`, change to bold-dark-blue `\textbf{\color{hustblue}备用定位源}` or simply remove the color markup. If it is a stray `\color{orange}`, delete it.

**[MEDIUM] FINDING 4 — P14: sparkline line colors may include orange**
The left sparkline ("持续微小注入") contains two lines — the upper line (攻击幅度) appears to use a warm/amber color. If this is `orange` or `TolOrange`, replace with `hustblue` or `gray` to stay within the palette. The right sparkline appears to use red for the post-threshold spike — red is acceptable as an alert color.

**[MEDIUM] FINDING 5 — P4: "攻击注入" node fill may differ from sibling nodes**
The 攻击注入 TikZ box on P4 appears to carry a slightly darker or differently-colored fill compared to the other four pipeline nodes. All five nodes should be uniform white fill (`fill=white`) with the same thin dark-blue border. Inspect the TikZ source and ensure no `fill=hustblue!10` or similar residue on that node.

**[LOW] FINDING 6 — P1: footer omits advisor name vs. reference convention**
The NEW deck footer shows only "王致尧 (HUST)" while the reference footer included the advisor ("答辩人：王致尧 指导教师：周纯杰 (HUST)"). This is a 毕业答辩 (final defense) rather than 开题答辩, so the format difference may be intentional — but confirm with the committee requirement whether advisor attribution is required in the footer.</result>

  "new_string": "% ===== 15 防护代价 =====\n\\begin{frame}\n  \\frametitle{防护代价：检测触发\\;\\textcolor{red!75!black}{\\ensuremath{\\neq}}\\;安全恢复}\n  \\begin{center}\\small\n  \\begin{tabular}{@{}lccl@{}}\n    \\toprule\n    攻击类型 & 是否被检测 & 严重失效降幅 & 综合 \\\\\n    \\midrule\n    GNSS 恒定偏置 & $\\checkmark$ & 15.4\\%\\;$\\to$\\;\\textbf{0.0\\%}  & 有效 \\\\\n    GNSS 渐进漂移 & $\\checkmark$ & 23.1\\%\\;$\\to$\\;\\textbf{15.4\\%} & 部分 \\\\\n    IMU 航向偏置  & \\textcolor{red!75!black}{\\textbf{$\\times$}} & 25.0\\%\\;$\\to$\\;\\textbf{25.0\\%} & \\textcolor{red!75!black}{\\textbf{未抑制}} \\\\\n    \\bottomrule\n  \\end{tabular}\\par\\vspace{0.4em}\n  {\\footnotesize\\itshape\\color{black!60}补充：角速率 CUSUM 在 77 次闭环测试中对预设 IMU 姿态攻击均\\textbf{ 5 秒内触发}\\\\\n  ——这是检测覆盖增强，不代表完整防护能力}\n  \\end{center}\n  \\vspace{0.4em}\n  \\begin{block}{结论}\n    \\bluehead{能检测到异常，不等于车辆已经安全恢复。}静态参数不能直接代表防护开启后的风险水平。\n  \\end{block}\n\\end{frame}"

  "old_string": "% ===== 16 总结 =====\n\\begin{frame}\n  \\frametitle{总结：四层评价口径才能反映真实风险}\n  \\begin{block}{核心贡献}\n    提出\\bluehead{“检测—拒绝—停车—任务”} 四层防护评价口径，并在统一仿真平台上系统量化了三类静态攻击与两类动态攻击在该口径下的差异。\\par\n    主要发现：\\bluehead{静态参数不能直接代表防护开启后的风险水平}——防护会改变攻击进入闭环后的表现，必须把检测、拒绝、停车与任务结果放在同一实验链路评价。\n  \\end{block}\n  \\vspace{0.4em}\n\n  \\bluehead{支撑工作}\n  \\begin{itemize}\n    \\item 构建 CARLA 闭环 + 攻击中间件 + 运行时防护的可复现仿真框架\n    \\item 在 54 实例 / 7 地图 / 8 模板上建立三类静态攻击的候选边界与跨场景规律\n  \\end{itemize}\n\\end{frame}",
  "new_string": "% ===== 16 总结 =====\n\\begin{frame}\n  \\frametitle{总结：四层评价口径才能反映真实风险}\n  \\begin{block}{核心贡献}\n    提出\\bluehead{“检测—拒绝—停车—任务”} 四层防护评价口径，并在统一仿真平台上系统量化了三类静态攻击与两类动态攻击在该口径下的差异。\\par\n    主要发现：\\bluehead{静态参数不能直接代表防护开启后的风险水平}——防护会改变攻击进入闭环后的表现，必须把检测、拒绝、停车与任务结果放在同一实验链路评价。\n  \\end{block}\n  \\vspace{0.6em}\n  \\noindent{\\color{black!30}\\rule{\\textwidth}{0.4pt}}\\par\n  \\vspace{0.3em}\n  {\\footnotesize\\color{black!70}\n  \\begin{itemize}\\setlength{\\itemsep}{0.15em}\n    \\item 构建 CARLA 闭环 + 攻击中间件 + 运行时防护的可复现仿真框架\n    \\item 在 54 实例 / 7 地图 / 8 模板上建立三类静态攻击的候选边界与跨场景规律\n  \\end{itemize}}\n\\end{frame}"

Here are the Round-2 verification results.

---

**Fix-by-Fix Verification**

1. **P1 footer** — VERIFIED. Footer reads "答辩人：王致尧 | 指导教师：周纯杰 (HUST)" with no duplication.

2. **P4 研究意义 in \block{}** — VERIFIED. The caption sits inside a proper teal-header block, consistent with P3's "本文切入点" block style.

3. **P8 frametitle** — VERIFIED. Reads "场景库覆盖三类道路几何，评价分四层指标" exactly.

4. **P10 table footnote** — VERIFIED. Short footnote reads "阈值 = 触发严重失效所需的最小攻击幅度；加粗 = 行内最小/最大". Clean single line.

5. **P11 red cells** — NOT VERIFIED. The "25.0%" and "未抑制" cells appear to render in the same weight/color as the surrounding body text — they read as bold black, not red. No visible red color on page 11.

6. **P13 gray supplementary row** — VERIFIED. The "屏蔽诊断维度补充 / 13.8%" row is visibly lighter/italic compared to the main PPO row, clearly differentiated.

7. **P14 curve labels / legend** — VERIFIED. No inline curve labels visible; "攻击幅度" (blue) and "轨迹误差" (red) legend lines appear in both blocks.

8. **P15 frametitle, 4-column table, red cells** — PARTIAL. Frametitle "防护代价：能检测，未必能安全恢复" — VERIFIED (no math symbol). Table is 4 columns — VERIFIED. Red cells for "25.0%" and "未抑制" — NOT VERIFIED: same issue as P11; these cells appear bold black, not red. The × mark for IMU row renders as a plain "×" in black.

9. **P16 hierarchy** — VERIFIED. Single prominent \block{核心贡献} at top; supporting bullet items below in smaller gray text.

10. **P17 frametitle** — VERIFIED. Reads "当前局限明确，后续路径已规划".

---

**Remaining Style Issues**

- **P11 & P15 red color not rendering**: The `\color{red}` or equivalent applied to "25.0%" / "未抑制" is not producing visible red in the compiled PDF. Both cells appear bold black. This is the most significant remaining bug — likely the color command is outside the cell scope or the table cell overrides it with `\bfseries` without a color reset. Check that `\textcolor{red}{\textbf{25.0\%}}` is used (not `{\color{red}\bfseries ...}` which can lose color in tabular mode with some beamer themes).

- **P17 "备用定位源" highlight**: The phrase "等备用定位源" on P17 is highlighted in teal/blue-green, which is visually inconsistent — it looks like a stray `\textcolor` that was not in the Round-1 change list. If intentional, it is the only colored inline phrase on that slide and looks like an editing artifact.

- **P9 slide**: "攻击类型" block header is missing — the three attack types (GNSS恒定偏置, GNSS渐进漂移, IMU航向偏置) appear directly under a bare bold label without a \block{} wrapper, inconsistent with P3/P4/P6 block style.

- **P18 (谢谢) footer**: Footer still shows "毕业答辩" in the center slot; P1 title slide shows "毕业答辩" as well, which is correct. No issue here.

**Summary**: Two fixes are incomplete — the red cell color on P11 and P15. The stray teal highlight on P17 and the missing \block{} on P9 are new issues to address.

I have now read all 18 new pages and the key reference pages. Here is the full audit.

---

## Slide Design Audit: new-01 through new-18

### Page-by-Page Report

**new-01 (Title)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. The dark-navy frametitle bar is absent (title slide correctly omits it), the blue title box and footer band match the reference. However the footer separator shows "答辩人: 王致尧 | 指导教师: 周纯杰 (HUST)" — the pipe character "|" is used instead of the em-dash or double-space spacing seen in the reference footer. Minor but inconsistent with ref-01.
- Density: clean — no issue.
- Hierarchy: "谢谢！" equivalent is the title; identifiable instantly. Yes.

**new-02 (汇报提纲)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Blue numbered balls and dark-navy frametitle bar present and correct. Only 4 items (vs ref-02's 5) — appropriate tightening. Clean.
- Density: 4 items, well-spaced. No violation.
- Hierarchy: Yes, outline structure clear at a glance.

**new-03 (研究背景)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Two rounded blocks with dark-blue title strip — correct. Bold dark-blue inline emphasis present. However the pipeline diagram ("场景 → 攻击 → 防护 → 闭环结果") sits sandwiched between the two blocks with no visual container, and the box borders on the pipeline nodes appear to use a lighter blue/gray rather than the consistent thin-border white-fill TikZ style described. The diagram floats somewhat unanchored.
- Density: 3 bullets in block 1, plus inline diagram, plus block 2 with multi-line text. Total content is at the edge — borderline 5-element overload.
- Hierarchy: The takeaway ("本文切入点") is in the bottom block, readable but below the fold of the eye-path. Marginally acceptable. Yes.

**new-04 (研究对象) — SPECIAL FOCUS**
- Style match: PARTIAL. The frametitle ("研究对象：定位—控制闭环") is correct, dark-navy bar, one diagram + one block. Split-attention is largely resolved — the diagram sits alone above the block without competing text. Good improvement.
- Issue: large vertical white space between frametitle and diagram (roughly one-third of slide height is blank), making the slide feel under-filled. The diagram nodes ("GNSS/IMU 观测", "攻击注入", "EKF 融合定位", "路径跟踪控制", "越界/碰撞轨迹偏离") are thin-border white-fill boxes — consistent. Sub-labels below each node in gray — good.
- Density: one diagram + one block. No violation. ONE primary visual primitive. Yes.
- Hierarchy: Yes, diagram is the immediate eye-catcher, block caption follows.
- Issue: The empty upper half creates awkward dead space. The diagram could be shifted up or the block enlarged.

**new-05 (现有问题与本文回答的三个问题)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Frametitle bar correct. Blue numbered enumerate inside rounded block — correct. Bold dark-blue inline emphasis on key phrases (最小有效边界, 静态攻击参数, 防护开启后). No orange visible.
- Density: Two inline sub-sections ("已有基础" / "仍待解决") with plain text above the block, then a block with 3 numbered items. This is 2 + 2 + 3 = borderline 7 total content pieces. The sub-headers "已有基础" and "仍待解决" are plain dark-blue text without a visual container — they float as freestanding lines.
- Hierarchy: The main block "本文回答的三个问题" is the single takeaway. Yes, identifiable.
- Issue: "已有基础" and "仍待解决" labels in the middle of the slide are stylistically orphaned — they look like colored plain text with no block wrapper, which creates inconsistency with the rest of the deck where such sections use block environments.

**new-06 (技术路线)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Correct frametitle, correct block. The pipeline diagram uses a two-row flow (1→2→3→4 then 7←6←5). This layout is clean. The block ("阶段说明") has bold inline emphasis. Consistent style.
- Density: one diagram + one block. No violation. Good.
- Hierarchy: Yes. The finding block anchors the takeaway.

**new-07 (实验平台)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Correct frametitle, no block used — instead uses a two-column layout with diagram left, "平台特性" heading and bullets right, and then an italic footnote below. The italic footnote text is a gray-ish tone, appropriately secondary.
- Density: diagram + 4 bullets + footnote. Acceptable. However the "平台特性" sub-header is plain text (dark blue, no block wrapper), inconsistent with pages 5, 9 etc. which sometimes use colored label text.
- Hierarchy: Yes.
- Issue: the right-column bullets wrap to two lines each (e.g., "攻击作为可插\n拔中间件"), creating a crowded column. The 4-bullet column appears tighter than the diagram.

**new-08 (场景库) — SPECIAL FOCUS**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Finding-style frametitle ("场景库覆盖三类道路几何，评价分四层指标") — excellent, this is the strongest frametitle in the deck. Booktabs table with bold values (54, 7, 8, 0.05s) — correct. "评价口径" block with dark-blue title strip — clean.
- Issue: There is a large blank region above the table — the table appears in the middle-lower half of the content area, leaving roughly 25% of content height blank above it. This dead space makes the slide feel poorly filled.
- Density: table (4 rows) + block (2 sentences). No violation.
- Hierarchy: Yes. Table rows with bold numerics draw the eye; block summarizes at bottom.

**new-09 (静态攻击实验设计)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Correct frametitle, correct block. Blue numbered enumerate with 4 items inside. "攻击类型" block below the numbered list contains three items on one line separated by bullets — functional but the block body is a single dense line with no visual breathing room.
- Density: Sub-header "实验目的" + "研究步骤" in plain colored text above the enumerate, then two blocks. Content is borderline heavy.
- Hierarchy: Yes, the numbered steps dominate.
- Issue: "实验目的" and "研究步骤" labels are again plain text (not inside a block), inconsistent with the pattern established in other slides where key labels are block-wrapped. Line "研究步骤" is not even visible as a separate label — it sits between the "实验目的" text and the enumeration.

**new-10 (静态结果) — SPECIAL FOCUS**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Booktabs table with bold values (0.51, 0.021, 5.0 for 弯道; 1.74, 0.057, 15.3 for 路口) — bold correctly applied to per-row min/max. Short gray italic footnote below table. "结论" block with dark-blue title strip and bold key phrase. All elements present.
- Density: table + footnote + block. No violation.
- Hierarchy: Bold numerics in table immediately draw attention, block confirms. Yes.
- Issue: The column header row shows "弯道 直道 路口" — the instruction says bold for 弯道 and 路口 (as they are the min/max), but the column headers themselves are NOT bolded — only the data values are bold. This is correct per the brief. Clean at distance. OK.
- Minor issue: the table is horizontally centered but left-aligned with wide left margin — the "攻击类型" column runs far left while numerics are center-right, creating a slightly awkward horizontal weight distribution. Not a critical defect.

**new-11 (防护回放) — SPECIAL FOCUS, RED CHECK**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Correct frametitle. Booktabs table, conclusion block.
- CRITICAL COLOR CHECK — "25.0%" and "未抑制": In the IMU row, the "防护后" column shows "25.0%" in RED (visually confirmed — clearly a different color from the black/gray of other numbers, distinctly red/crimson). The "综合" column shows "未抑制" also in RED. Both are unambiguously red at 180 DPI. PASS.
- The "15.4%" in GNSS 渐进漂移 row appears in bold dark (black/dark-blue), and "0.0%" in GNSS 恒定偏置 row is bold black — these are NOT red, which is correct (only the un-suppressed IMU row should be red).
- Density: table (3 rows × 4 cols) + footnote + block. No violation.
- Hierarchy: The red IMU row pops visually and the conclusion block confirms. Yes, clear in under 3 seconds.

**new-12 (动态攻击设计)**
- Style match: Correct frametitle. Diagram (3-node cycle: PPO策略 → CARLA闭环 → 奖励 → back) sits alone above block. "奖励设计" block with bold inline "幅度初始化". Clean.
- Density: one diagram + one block. No violation.
- Hierarchy: Yes.
- Issue: The diagram circle-flow layout is clear but the "奖励：失效 + 隐蔽 – 检测" box is notably smaller than the other two nodes, and its text has a line break that creates uneven sizing. Minor visual imbalance.

**new-13 (动态结果) — SPECIAL FOCUS**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Booktabs table, conclusion block.
- 13.8% row: The "屏蔽诊断维度补充" row is rendered in italic gray — visually clearly lighter/grayed vs the main black rows above. The 13.8% value is also in italic. PASS — the row is visibly grayed.
- Density: table (3 rows) + footnote + block. No violation.
- Hierarchy: The bold "50.1%" in PPO主实验row immediately draws the eye. Block confirms. Yes.
- Issue: The frametitle "动态攻击：检测率从 99.4% 降至 50.1%" is an excellent finding-style title. The column header row has "检测率 严重失效率 备注" — all in normal weight, booktabs style correct. Good.

**new-14 (动态行为) — SPECIAL FOCUS**
- Style match: PARTIAL. The two-column layout with one block per column, each containing a sparkline-style chart plus a caption label below, works reasonably.
- Charts: both show two lines in distinct colors (dark blue = 攻击幅度, red/salmon = 轨迹误差). Small legends inside each chart ("–攻击幅度 –轨迹误差") in the chart area.
- Density: 2 blocks, each self-contained. Below the blocks, a "共同点" paragraph with bold "CUSUM 累积门槛" inline. No enumeration overload.
- Hierarchy: The two charts provide immediate visual contrast. "共同点" label draws the shared insight. Yes.
- Issue: The "共同点：" label below the blocks appears as plain text (blue bold label + plain body), NOT inside a block wrapper, inconsistent with the block-for-conclusions pattern used on pages 10, 11, 13, 15, 16. This is a style inconsistency.
- Issue: The legend text in the mini charts ("–攻击幅度 –轨迹误差") is very small at slide scale. At projection distance this will likely be illegible. The sparklines themselves are sufficient to convey the trend, but legend labels risk being too tiny.

**new-15 (防护代价) — SPECIAL FOCUS, RED CHECK + MATH GLYPH**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Frametitle: "防护代价：能检测，未必能安全恢复" — no broken math glyph visible. The colon is a normal ASCII colon, text is clean. PASS on math glyph.
- RED CHECK — "未抑制": The IMU row "综合" column shows "未抑制" in RED. Visually confirmed — it is the same red as on page 11. PASS.
- Additional red check: The "25.0% → 25.0%" in the "严重失效降幅" column for IMU row — the "25.0%" values are in dark text (not red), which is correct per the design (only "未抑制" is the red highlight).
- Footnote: a two-line italic gray footnote below the table. The second line "──这是检测覆盖增强，不代表完整防护能力" uses an em-dash correctly. Clean.

- Hierarchy: Yes. The "×" in IMU row and red "未抑制" pop immediately. Block confirms.

**new-16 (总结) — SPECIAL FOCUS**
- Style match: PARTIAL. "核心贡献" block fills the upper portion with the hero statement in bold italics ("检测—拒绝—停车—任务") inside double quotes. Below the block, a thin gray rule separates it from two bullet points acting as supporting detail.
- Issue: The "核心贡献" block body is VERY dense — four lines of text including the main claim AND the supporting 主要发现 paragraph, all crammed inside one block. The block text wraps to 4+ lines; this is too dense for a hero block. The main takeaway sentence is buried in the middle of a paragraph. A hero block should have ONE punchy sentence; the 主要发现 paragraph should be outside the block or in a second lighter block.
- Issue: The thin rule and the two supporting bullets below it ("构建CARLA闭环…" and "在54实例/7地图…") are present as expected. However both bullet items exceed 18 Chinese characters per line (each runs ~25–30 chars including Latin). Density violation.
- Hierarchy: The bold-quoted "检测—拒绝—停车—任务" is the intended hero phrase but it is not the FIRST thing the eye lands on — the block title "核心贡献" competes, and the dense paragraph body delays comprehension. Marginally No for the 3-second test.

**new-17 (当前局限明确，后续路径已规划)**
- Style match: PARTIAL. Two side-by-side blocks ("当前不足" / "后续工作"), each with the dark-blue title strip. Symmetric layout, 3 bullets each. The "备用定位源" inline text at the end of the last bullet is in blue bold, consistent with the inline emphasis pattern.
- Density: 3 bullets per block, all readable lengths. No violation.
- Hierarchy: The two-block split makes the takeaway self-evident. Yes.
- Issue: The two blocks appear directly adjacent with very little gutter between them (the gap is narrower than the padding inside). At projection this may appear as one wide merged box.

**new-18 (谢谢)**
- Style match: Correct. Matches ref-23 exactly in structure.
- Hierarchy: Yes.

---

## Critical Color Findings

- new-11: "25.0%" (IMU 防护后) — RED. CONFIRMED. PASS.
- new-11: "未抑制" (IMU 综合) — RED. CONFIRMED. PASS.
- new-15: "未抑制" (IMU 综合) — RED. CONFIRMED. PASS.
- new-15: Math glyph in title — NO broken glyph. PASS.

---

## Concrete Fix List (Severity Order)

**1. CRITICAL — new-16: Hero block is too dense; supporting paragraph must be extracted.**
The "核心贡献" block contains both the hero claim and a 主要发现 paragraph (~60 chars, 4 lines). Split into: (a) block body = one short bold sentence only; (b) 主要发现 paragraph either outside the block as plain indented text or in a second lighter block. The two bottom bullets also exceed 18 chars/line and must be shortened or broken into sub-lines.

**2. HIGH — new-16: Supporting bullets violate 18-char/line limit.**
Both bullets below the rule are ~25–30 Chinese chars per line. Shorten to ≤18 chars by splitting into sub-items or rewording. Example: "构建 CARLA 闭环可复现框架 / 含攻击中间件与运行时防护" as two sub-lines.

**3. HIGH — new-04: Excessive dead space above the diagram.**
The diagram sits in the lower 60% of the content area, leaving ~35% of blank vertical space above it. Either shift diagram upward toward the frametitle or add a one-line "研究意义" sub-header above the diagram to fill the visual gap.

**4. HIGH — new-14: "共同点" conclusion not wrapped in a block.**
Unlike every other content slide, the key takeaway on new-14 appears as loose plain text ("共同点：…") without a block wrapper. Wrap in a rounded "结论" or "共同点" block with dark-blue title strip, consistent with pages 10–13, 15–16.

**5. MEDIUM — new-05 and new-09: Floating sub-headers ("已有基础" / "仍待解决" / "实验目的" / "研究步骤") are stylistically orphaned.**
These plain colored text labels are not inside blocks and look visually stranded. Either promote them to block titles (each wrapping their subordinate bullets) or demote them to bold inline text on the first bullet.

**6. MEDIUM — new-08 and new-04: Dead space above the booktabs table / diagram.**
On new-08, roughly 25% of content area is blank above the table. Pull the table toward the top of the content region or add the frametitle-finding label as a second line inside the frametitle bar.

**7. MEDIUM — new-14: Sparkline legends are illegibly small.**
The "–攻击幅度 –轨迹误差" legends inside the mini charts will be unreadable at projection distance. Move the legend below each chart as a caption line, or increase font size to at least 7pt equivalent.

**8. MEDIUM — new-17: Block gutter too narrow between "当前不足" and "后续工作".**
Add \hspace or increase columnsep so the two adjacent blocks have a visible gap (at least 0.5em) rather than appearing merged.

**9. LOW — new-03: Pipeline diagram ("场景 → 攻击 → 防护 → 闭环结果") has no visual container.**
The four boxes float between the two content blocks with no background rectangle or bounding frame. Add a light-gray background tcolorbox behind the pipeline or at minimum a thin-border white-fill outer rectangle, consistent with the TikZ container style used on new-04 and new-06.

**10. LOW — new-01: Footer separator is "|" (pipe) instead of reference style.**
ref-01 uses a double-space layout. The "|" is not wrong but breaks consistency with the reference. Change "答辩人: 王致尧 | 指导教师:" to match ref-01's em-space or thin-space separator.

**11. LOW — new-12: Diagram node size imbalance.**
The "奖励" node in the PPO cycle diagram is smaller than the other two nodes. Make all three nodes the same minimum-width box.

**new-04 "完整链路：" sub-line** — VERIFIED. The blue bold "完整链路：" label with the full pipeline description appears above the diagram, filling the previous dead space cleanly.

**new-12 4-node equal-sized PPO diagram** — NOT VERIFIED. The diagram has 4 nodes but they are visibly unequal in size: "观测 状态/防护统计/攻击历史" is roughly twice the height of "PPO 策略" and "CARLA 闭环 (防护开启)", and "奖励 失效+隐蔽-检测" is also oversized. The nodes are not equal-sized.

**new-14 "共同点" in \block{} + \scriptsize legends** — VERIFIED. "共同点" is rendered inside a dark-header block consistent with other slides, and the sparkline color legends ("攻击幅度 轨迹误差") are visibly small (scriptsize-equivalent). The left-right column gutter between the two panels is present and clear.

**new-16 核心贡献 block with one sentence, 主要发现 outside above rule** — VERIFIED. The block contains one sentence. "主要发现" with two short bullets sits outside/above the rule, and two supporting bullets appear below the rule in condensed form.

**new-17 visible gutter + condensed bullets (<=14 chars)** — PARTIALLY VERIFIED. The gutter between the two columns is visible. However, the right column "后续工作" has one bullet that wraps to a second line ("引入备用定位源（VO、高精 / 地图）"), indicating the text exceeds the 14-char condensed target. Left column bullets are short.

---

**Production Readiness: Yes, with one note**

The deck is production-ready for an undergrad defense. Content is logically sequenced, all key findings are stated with quantified numbers, honest negative findings are acknowledged (IMU attack not suppressed, PPO stability limited), and no overclaiming is present.

**One potentially embarrassing issue:** new-12 PPO closed-loop diagram — the "观测" node is roughly 2x taller than the other three. This asymmetry looks like a layout bug mid-presentation and may draw a committee question about whether the diagram is intentional.

**Style match score vs reference: 8/10.** The HUST Beamer theme, color palette, block structure, footer, and typography are consistent throughout; deductions for the unequal PPO nodes on new-12 and one wrapped bullet on new-17 that breaks the visual rhythm.

  "prompt": "You are a senior typographer / design critic auditing a defense-grade Beamer deck. Apply the HIGHEST standard. Use your multimodal vision — Read each PNG as an image; do not skip or skim.\n\nNEW DECK (18 pages at 200 DPI):\n/tmp/slide-png/new-01.png … /tmp/slide-png/new-18.png\n\nREFERENCE (visual gold standard, 23 pages):\n/tmp/slide-png/ref-01.png … /tmp/slide-png/ref-23.png — sample 6+ pages to internalize.\n\nThe deck has already been through 2 review rounds and matches the reference at the structural level (Madrid + whale colortheme, no orange, booktabs, bare blocks). Now I need DEEP POLISH — the kind a typographer would do before printing for a committee.\n\nApply these strict criteria, page by page:\n\n1. **Vertical balance**: every page must fill the visual canvas. No empty thirds. No floating-low content. No floating-high titles. The visual mass should distribute across the body area.\n\n2. **Single focal point**: a viewer at projection distance must identify the page's ONE message in ≤ 2 seconds. All other elements should support, not compete.\n\n3. **Visual rhythm**: across the 18 pages, similar types of pages (e.g. the 5 table pages, the 4 diagram pages) should have consistent rhythm — same vertical placement, same spacing above/below tables.\n\n4. **Bullet discipline**: NO line wraps in any bullet. Every bullet must fit one line. (Look hard — even one wrapped char is a failure.)\n\n5. **Block usage discipline**: when a page has multiple \"labels\" (e.g. P5 \"已有基础\" / \"仍待解决\"; P9 \"实验目的\" / \"研究步骤\") — ALL labels must use the same primitive (all blocks, OR all bold sub-headers — never mixed within a page).\n\n6. **Diagram quality**: nodes at uniform size; arrows consistent thickness; spacing equal; labels at consistent distance.\n\n7. **Frametitle phrasing**: every title a finding, not a topic. ≤ 22 Chinese chars. No colons followed by topic noun phrases like \"研究对象：...\" UNLESS the second part is itself a finding.\n\n8. **Footnote / supplementary text**: italic gray below tables/charts. Consistent style across pages 7, 10, 11, 13, 15.\n\n9. **Color economy**: at most 3 colors per page — black body, dark blue (theme), red emphasis. Every other use is wrong.\n\n10. **End-of-frame whitespace**: bottom padding above the footer should be visually balanced with top padding below the frametitle.\n\nFor EACH page (1-18), give:\n- ONE-LINE verdict (POLISHED / NEEDS-WORK / BROKEN)\n- IF needs work: specific concrete fix (with exact what-to-change instruction)\n\nThen top of report: name the 5 most impactful improvements, ranked.\n\nBe RUTHLESS. If something looks even slightly amateur, flag it. Aim for the standard of a published academic conference keynote, not a student slide deck.\n\nWord budget: 700."
