tool_result Agent
```
---

## Page 1 (Title)
🔴 **Excessive dead space below date.** The title block, author, institute, and date occupy roughly the upper 60% of the frame; the bottom 35% is a blank white void before the footline. The vertical centering of the title block itself looks good, but the \vfill between the title block and footline collapses unevenly. The Madrid titlepage layout does not distribute vertical space evenly when content is sparse — add `\vspace*{1.5em}` before `\titlepage` or wrap in a `\vbox to \textheight{\vfill\titlepage\vfill}` environment to center the block vertically.

---

## Page 2 (TOC)
🔴 **Large dead zone top and bottom.** Four items with `\vspace{0.85em}` between them occupy about 40% of the body height; the top ~25% and bottom ~20% are empty. The `\vfill … \tableofcontents … \vfill` should distribute correctly, but the TOC template's own `\par\vspace{0.85em}` padding inflates item height without giving back the surrounding vfill space. Fix: reduce `\vspace{0.85em}` on line 62 to `\vspace{0.5em}`, and add an explicit `\vspace*{1em}` before the first item to push content toward vertical center.

✅ Circled number bullets render correctly, sizing and color are on-theme.

---

## Page 3 (Background)
✅ Overall balance is good — three zones (block + TikZ + block) fill the frame well.

🔴 **TikZ node "闭环结果" is noticeably wider than the other three nodes.** All four boxes use `minimum width=2.0cm` but the four-character label "闭环结果" forces the node wider than "场景" or "攻击", creating uneven box widths in the row. Fix: set `minimum width=2.2cm` uniformly or give all labels two CJK characters + explicit `\\` line break so all boxes stay equal width.

🔴 **CJK letter-spacing in the "做法" block.** The sentence starting "在 CARLA 仿真平台中…" has a noticeable space before CARLA but no spaces between the surrounding CJK characters, creating a micro-rhythm inconsistency. Not a critical issue but visible at projection resolution.

---

## Page 4 (Research Object)
✅ Two-column block proportions are well-matched.

🔴 **TikZ pipeline labels are misaligned vertically.** The `\node[lab,below=5pt of …]` sub-labels ("传感链", "威胁面", etc.) sit at different vertical positions because the parent boxes have two-line CJK labels of varying depth ("GNSS/IMU\n观测" vs. "攻击注入"). The `below=5pt` anchors to the box south but the box heights differ. Fix: replace `below=5pt of X` with `below=5pt of X.south` and set a fixed `text depth=0pt` on all `lab` nodes, or add `baseline` anchoring via `yshift` to normalize.

---

## Page 5 (Related Work / Research Questions)
🔴 **Block height mismatch in the top two-column row.** The left "已有基础" block has two bullets that each wrap onto a second line ("CARLA 支撑闭环驾驶仿真已\n成熟" and "GNSS 欺骗与 IMU 偏置已有\n讨论"), while the right "尚未充分回答" block has two shorter bullets that fit in single lines. This makes the left block visibly ~30% taller. Fix: use `[T]` alignment (already present) but equalize by either un-wrapping the left bullets or adding a `\phantom{}` line in the right block.

✅ The "待回答的三个问题" block fills the lower zone cleanly.

---

## Page 6 (Technical Roadmap)
🔴 **TikZ snake layout gap.** Nodes 1–4 form the top row; nodes 5–7 form the bottom row (right-to-left). The vertical gap between rows (`below=0.65cm of s4`) is noticeably tighter than the horizontal gap between columns (`right=0.32cm`), producing an asymmetric snake shape. The turn arrow from s4 down to s5 is visually short compared to the horizontal arrows. Fix: increase `below=0.65cm` to `below=0.85cm` for a more balanced turn.

🔴 **Footnote position inconsistency.** The gray footnote on line 207 ("PSO 结果是当前参数设置下…") uses `\hspace*{1em}` left-indent and sits between the block and `\vfill`, making it float awkwardly in the middle of the lower whitespace rather than anchoring to the bottom. Fix: move the footnote inside a `\footnotetext{}`-style construction or pin it with `\vspace*{\fill}` above it so it presses to the bottom margin just above the footline.

---

## TOP 3 Highest-Impact Visual Fixes

**1. Page 2 / TOC dead zones (line 62):** Reduce `\vspace{0.85em}` to `\vspace{0.5em}`. This is the most projected-upon slide and the excessive blank space at top and bottom looks unfinished. Combined with centering the block content properly, this will immediately sharpen the professionalism of the deck's entry slide.

**2. Page 5 / Column block height mismatch (lines 150–165):** The left "已有基础" block is ~30% taller than the right "尚未充分回答" block due to line wrapping. The fix is simple — either shorten the left bullet text or use `\begin{columns}[c]` with manually equalized content — and has the highest visual impact because asymmetric side-by-side blocks look like a layout error.

**3. Page 6 / Floating footnote (line 207):** The `\hspace*{1em}` footnote between the block and a `\vfill` drifts into the middle of the lower whitespace. Anchoring it immediately below the block with `\vspace{0.2em}` (no trailing `\vfill`) or pinning it to the footline via `\vspace*{\fill}` above it will remove the visual "floating label" artifact that is immediately noticeable.

Now I have everything needed. Here is the review:

---

## Page 7
🔴
1. **TikZ diagram — massive dead zone below nodes.** The pipeline row sits in the top third of the white area; the gap between the diagram and the blocks below it is ~40% of the slide body. `\vfill` distributes space but the diagram itself is undersized (node `minimum height=0.85cm`, `minimum width=1.95cm`). Increase node sizes (height to 1.1cm, width to 2.2cm) or reduce the overall `node distance` and bring the feedback arc tighter so the diagram takes up more vertical real-estate.
2. **TikZ feedback arc label missing.** The dashed return arrow carries no label — contrast P12 which labels every edge. Either add a label ("车辆状态") or visually distinguish the arc with a different dash pattern.
3. **Column block height mismatch — minor.** Left block (3 bullets) and right block (3 bullets) look balanced. No issue.

## Page 8
🔴
1. **Severe top dead zone.** The table sits roughly at 38% from the top. The `\vfill` above the table and below the `\frametitle` creates a large empty band at the top. Move the table to start immediately after the frametitle with a modest `\vspace{0.6em}` instead of `\vfill`.
2. **Column block height mismatch.** Left block (严重失效判定) has ~2 lines of text; right block (防护四层指标) wraps to 3 lines, making it visibly taller. Adjust font size or rebalance text so blocks are equal height.
3. **Table horizontal centering with narrow content.** The `lcl` table is narrow relative to the slide width, looking orphaned. Use `tabularx` with `\linewidth` to stretch it to match the block width below.

## Page 9
✅
1. **Block height mismatch.** Left block (步骤, 4 enum items) is significantly taller than right block (攻击类型, 3 bullets). The height difference is ~25%. Either add a blank item or padding to the right block, or switch to `[c]` column alignment so they appear anchored at center.

## Page 10
🔴
1. **Top dead zone.** Same pattern as P8 — `\vfill` before the table pushes it down, creating a large blank band at top. Fix: replace leading `\vfill` with `\vspace{0.5em}`.
2. **Bold emphasis in the conclusion block** uses `\bluehead{}` (structure color dark blue), which renders well. No color issue.
3. **Footnote color `black!70`** is noticeably gray but acceptable. Consistent with P11.

## Page 11
🔴
1. **Top dead zone** — same `\vfill` issue as P8/P10.
2. **Red color (`red!75!black`) for "25.0%" and "未抑制"** is off-theme (theme is navy/dark-blue/whale). This is the only orange-violating color but red also conflicts. Replace with `\textcolor{structure.fg}{\textbf{...}}` (dark blue bold) or use a darker crimson consistent with academic warning conventions, or simply bold + underline.
3. **"综合" column values** (有效抑制, 部分抑制, 未抑制) have inconsistent visual weight — first two are unformatted, last is bold+red. Consider making all three bold with differentiated colors from the theme palette.

## Page 12
🔴
1. **TikZ label clipping — "状态/统计" truncated.** The label above the obs→policy arrow reads "状态 / 统计" but is visually cut off at the right side of the node because `yshift=1pt` only and no `text width` is set. Set `text width=2cm,text centered` on the `lab` style.
2. **Left block line-break mid-phrase.** "观测 = 车辆状态 / 防护统计 / 攻击历史" wraps after "攻击" producing a dangling "历史" on line 2, and the inline `\,=\,` math spacing looks inconsistent with the surrounding CJK. Use `\newline` explicitly or shorten to fit one line.
3. **Block height mismatch.** Left block (2 lines) vs right block (2 lines) — appears roughly equal, but right block baseline sits lower due to the `\bluehead` inline formatting adding vertical space on the left. Minor.

---

## TOP 3 Highest-Impact Visual Fixes

1. **Remove leading `\vfill` on P8, P10, P11** — replace with `\vspace{0.5em}`. This eliminates the dominant empty-top-band artifact that makes all three data slides look unfinished. Single-line fix per frame, highest visual return.

2. **P12 TikZ label clipping ("状态/统计")** — add `text width=1.8cm,align=center` to the `lab` style. The truncated label on a diagram slide is immediately obvious and looks like a rendering error.

3. **P11 red color for "未抑制"** — replace `\textcolor{red!75!black}` with `\textcolor{structure.fg}` (theme navy bold). Red is the only off-palette color across all six slides and breaks the visual consistency of the entire deck.

- **Excessive dead space above table**: ~35% of the slide body is blank white between the frametitle bar and the first table rule. The table+block should be shifted up ~1.5cm or `\vspace` trimmed.
- **Red bold in conclusion block**: the `100%` highlight uses red, which is off-theme (no orange/red allowed). Replace with `\textbf` in the same dark-navy or use a mid-blue accent.
- **Table footnote alignment**: footnote text sits left-flush but is slightly indented inward relative to the table left edge — it should align exactly with column 1.

## Page 14
🔴
- **Large dead gap above the two sparkline blocks**: roughly 30% empty space between frametitle and the block row. Pull blocks up.
- **Sparkline axis arrows are very faint** — the arrowhead and axis lines barely register at presentation scale. Increase stroke to at least 0.6pt.
- **Threshold dashed label "失效阈值"** renders at ~7pt and sits uncomfortably close to the dashed line with no horizontal offset; needs `xshift=3pt` nudge.
- **Legend line swatches** in the left panel are monochrome blue/dark on a pale block background — legible, acceptable.

## Page 15
🔴
- **Red bold text in conclusion block** ("未抑制", arrows in 综合 column): same off-theme red as P13. Switch to the theme's dark-navy bold.
- **Two-column blocks are height-mismatched**: left block is ~40% taller than right block, creating a ragged bottom edge. Set both to the same `\vbox to <height>` or add `\vfill` inside the shorter right block.
- **Dead space above table**: still ~25% blank above the booktabs header. Add `\vspace{-0.8em}` or reduce pre-block skip.

## Page 16
✅ / 🔴
- Whitespace above the 核心结论 block is reduced vs. earlier rounds but there is still ~20% dead top. Minor but noticeable.
- **Two-column bottom blocks are visibly unequal height**: right block "实验层面" is shorter (2 bullets wrap) vs. left (2 short bullets). The ragged bottom breaks visual symmetry — equalize with `minipage` of fixed height.
- **Bullet in right column wraps mid-word** ("著失效" dangling on its own line) — the line break after "显" reads as awkward; widen the minipage by 1–2% or rephrase to fit.

## Page 17
🔴
- **Severe dead top**: roughly 45% of the slide body above the two blocks is blank white. This is the most egregious whitespace issue across all six slides. The block pair should be vertically centered or pushed up significantly (`\vspace{-2cm}` equivalent or `\vfill` removed).
- **No visual element above blocks**: a decorative rule or the block pair itself should sit at ~30% from the top of the body area, not 50%+.
- Column heights are balanced (3 vs 3 bullets) — that is good.

## Page 18
✅ / 🔴
- **谢谢 vertical position is too high** — the two characters sit in the upper half of the slide; the group (谢谢 + subtitle + info line) should be centered on the full slide, which would shift it ~0.8cm lower.
- **Separator in info line**: the centered dot `·` is correct and readable; no issue there.
- **谢谢 letter spacing**: the manual inter-character space (em-space between characters) looks proportional and consistent — acceptable.
- No off-theme colors — the dark-navy serif for 谢谢 is on-theme.

---

## TOP 3 Highest-Impact Fixes

1. **P17 dead top (highest impact)** — ~45% blank above blocks. Add `\vspace*{\fill}` before and after the column pair, or replace the fixed-skip with `\vfill`. Visually the most jarring slide in the deck.

2. **P13/P15 off-theme red** — Bold red (`\textcolor{red}` or similar) on `100%` and `未抑制` violates the no-red/no-orange rule. Replace with `\textcolor{structure}{\textbf{...}}` (the theme's dark-navy) throughout both slides.

3. **P13/P14/P15 dead top gaps** — All three slides lose 25–35% of body height to blank space above content. A single global reduction of the `\vspace` or `\bigskip` separating frametitle from first block — approximately `\setlength{\beamertopskip}{0.4cm}` or equivalent — would tighten all three at once.

Now I have enough to write the review.

---

## Page 1 (Title)
✅ Clean. Title block is well-sized, subtitle sits tightly beneath, author/institute/date lines have good rhythm. No issue.

## Page 2 (TOC)
🔴 The four TOC items occupy only the top ~60% of the text area; the bottom 40% is dead white space. The `\vfill … \tableofcontents … \vfill` sandwich is not expanding evenly because `\tableofcontents` has a fixed internal skip. Fix: replace `\vspace{0.85em}` between items with `\vspace{1.6em}` (roughly double), or wrap in a minipage with explicit height and spread via `\vspace{\stretch{1}}` separators, so all four items fill the frame vertically.

## Page 3 (背景)
🔴 The TikZ pipeline strip (场景→攻击→防护→闭环结果) sits too far from the block above it — the gap is ~30% of the frame height, much looser than the gap between the strip and the block below. The `\vfill` triplet distributes all slack equally, but the pipeline has no vertical weight, so it visually "floats" in the middle third. Fix: replace the middle `\vfill` (above the tikzpicture) with `\vspace{0.5em}` and the one below it with `\vspace{0.3em}` to anchor the diagram tighter to both blocks.

## Page 4 (研究对象)
🔴 The two bottom blocks are visibly unequal in height: 研究意义 is shorter (~2 lines) while 研究边界 wraps to 3 lines. With `[T]` alignment the gap below 研究意义 is conspicuous. Fix: add `\vspace*{\fill}` inside the 研究意义 block, or equalize with `\begin{block}...\vphantom{不涉及感知}\end{block}` phantom to force equal minimum height.

## Page 5 (现有研究)
🔴 Same block-height inequality as P4: the left column (2 bullets) renders noticeably shorter than the right (2 bullets but with a bold wrap). The `\vfill` between column-pair and the 待回答 block is large. Fix: same phantom/vfill technique inside the shorter block. Additionally the `\vfill` above the columns is slightly over-generous — tighten to `\vspace{0.6em}`.

## Page 6 (技术路线)
✅ The snake diagram is well-spaced and the turn from step 4 to 5 reads cleanly. The disclaimer footnote in gray is appropriately sized. No issue.

## Page 7 (实验平台)
🔴 The dashed feedback arrow from 路径跟踪 back to GNSS/IMU curves through a large loop, pushing the CARLA 仿真车辆 node far below the main pipeline — about 1 cm of empty white space sits between the pipeline row and the feedback node. The `below=1.0cm` combined with the `\vfill` above gives excessive sky between frametitle and the diagram. Fix: reduce `below=1.0cm` to `below=0.65cm` and reduce the `looseness=1.2` to `looseness=0.9` on the feedback curve to flatten the arc.

## Page 8 (场景库)
🔴 The booktabs table is horizontally narrow (~55% of text width) and left-aligned rather than centered, making the right half of the slide blank above the two blocks. The `\begin{center}` should produce centered output, but the `@{}` column specifiers remove padding and the table collapses inward. Fix: replace `@{}lcl@{}` with `lp{1.4cm}l` inside a `tabularx{\textwidth}` or explicitly set `\setlength\tabcolsep{8pt}` and drop `@{}` so the table stretches naturally to ~70% width.

## Page 9 (静态攻击)
✅ Layout is clean. The 目的 full-width block, then two-column 步骤/攻击类型, has balanced heights (4 bullets vs 3 bullets — close enough). No issue.

---

## TOP 3 Highest-Impact Fixes

**1. P2 TOC vertical distribution** — Items cluster in the top half leaving a large white void. Double the inter-item `\vspace` to `1.6em` or use `\vspace{\stretch{1}}` between entries to fill the full frame. Most visually jarring slide in the deck.

**2. P4 / P5 twin-block height imbalance** — Unequal column-block heights (shorter left, taller right) recur on both pages. Add a `\vphantom{}` in the shorter block matching a long string from the taller block, or switch to `[c]` column alignment with a uniform `\vspace{\fill}` after each block body. Affects two slides.

**3. P8 table too narrow** — The booktabs table occupies only ~55% of slide width, leaving conspicuous right-side blank space. Switch to `tabularx{\linewidth}` with an `X` column for the 说明 field, or remove `@{}` and widen `\tabcolsep` to push the table to 70–75% width.

```
Now I have a complete picture. Here is the visual-only review:

---

## Page 10
✅ Frametitle fits on one line. Table columns align cleanly (booktabs rules visible). Grey alternating rows absent but not required here. The `结论` block sits low with visible dead whitespace above the table — the `\vfill` above the table and below the block creates three roughly equal zones; acceptable but the table floats high leaving ~25% empty band. No word-wrap issues.

## Page 11
✅ Same layout pattern as P10. Red "未抑制" and red "25.0%" are intentional and render cleanly at appropriate weight. Table footnote text is visually light but consistent with other slides. Column alignment correct.

## Page 12
🔴 **Block column height mismatch.** Left block (观测与奖励) is visibly taller than right block (对照设置) — the right block ends ~1.5 lines short, leaving a blank gap inside the right block's bottom. This is because the left block has two `\item` lines that wrap slightly. Fix: add `\vspace*{\fill}` inside right block before `\end{block}`, or switch to `[c]` column alignment with a fixed `\vbox to <height>` wrapper.

## Page 13
✅ Clean. Three-row table, all columns left-aligned except numeric columns which are visually consistent. Greyed-out third row renders at the right visual weight (lighter text). No wraps, footnote styling matches P10/P11.

## Page 14
🔴 **Sparkline axis arrows too thin / nearly invisible at presentation resolution.** The vertical Y-axis stroke and the arrowhead on both panels are rendered at default `\draw` line width (~0.4pt in TikZ at this scale), making them hard to read under projector conditions. Fix: add `line width=0.6pt` to the axis draw calls. Additionally, the "失效阈值" label in both panels is tight against the dashed line with no horizontal gap — add `xshift=2pt` or `\hspace{2pt}` in the node anchor. Left and right sparkline blocks are visually equal height — no column imbalance.

## Page 15
✅ Two-column block layout (结论 + 补充:角速率 CUSUM) has slightly unequal heights — the right block is ~2 lines shorter — but the gap is minor and within tolerance. Red ×, "未抑制" are intentional. Checkmarks render clean. No wraps.

## Page 16
🔴 **Content cluster dead-top.** The `核心结论` block starts immediately after the frametitle bar with almost no padding, while a large dead zone (~30% of frame height) sits between the two-column blocks and the footer. The three `\vfill` tokens (line 536, 540, 560) are distributing space correctly in principle, but the 核心结论 block is taller than the column blocks, so the `\vfill` before it collapses. Fix: replace the leading `\vfill` (line 536) with `\vspace{1.2em}` to guarantee a visible top margin.

## Page 17
🔴 **Severe dead-bottom.** The two-column blocks occupy only the upper ~40% of the usable frame area. Both `\vfill` tokens (lines 566, 588) surround the single `\begin{columns}` block, meaning all the slack space pools below the blocks. The visual impression is that content is floating at the top. Fix: wrap content in `\vbox to \textheight{\vfill ... \vfill}` or add `\vspace{3em}` before the columns to push the block toward vertical center, e.g., replace line 566 `\vfill` with `\vspace{0.18\textheight}`.

## Page 18
🔴 **Info line separator inconsistency.** The `·` (centered dot) used as separator in the info line (王致尧 · 华中科技大学… · 2026年5月) is produced by `\;$\cdot$\;` which renders the dot at math-axis height rather than text-midline — visually the dot sits slightly low compared to the surrounding Chinese characters. Fix: replace `$\cdot$` with `{\textbullet}` at `\small` scale, or use the Unicode middle dot `·` directly, which aligns properly with CJK glyphs. Additionally, the vertical positioning of the entire block is slightly above true center — the `\vfill` is inside `\begin{center}...\end{center}` which clips its effect; move `\vfill` outside the center environment or use `\centering` + a `minipage`.

---

## TOP 3 Highest-Impact Fixes

1. **P17 dead-bottom (highest impact):** Replace `\vfill` at line 566 with `\vspace{0.18\textheight}` so the two-column block lands at visual center rather than the top third. This is the most jarring layout defect across all 9 pages.

2. **P16 dead-top:** Replace `\vfill` at line 536 with `\vspace{1.2em}` to give the 核心结论 block a proper top margin and prevent it from pressing against the frametitle bar.

3. **P14 sparkline axis weight:** Add `line width=0.6pt` to the TikZ axis draw commands in both sparkline panels, and add `xshift=3pt` to the "失效阈值" label nodes to prevent label-line collision. These sparklines are the only figures in the deck; thin axes undercut their visual authority on a projected screen.

TikZ aesthetic: node sizing/spacing, arrow elegance, label clarity, CJK letter-spacing (字 间 距 in 2-char labels)\n4. Table widths, column alignment, footnote styling\n5. Vertical content distribution\n6. Bullet wrap mid-word\n7. Inter-element spacing rhythm\n\nThis is round 4 of iteration. Don't invent issues.\n\nFor each page:\n```\n## Page N\n✅ or 🔴\n[concrete issue + fix]\n```\n\nEnd with TOP 2 priority fixes for these 9 pages. Maximum 400 words."

> TOOL

tool_result Agent
```
## Page 1
Clean title slide. The gap above the title banner and below the date is generous but intentional for a title frame.

## Page 2
The four TOC items sit in the lower ~60% of the frame with a large blank band at the top. The vertical distribution is noticeably bottom-heavy — the white space above item 1 is roughly double the space between items. This is the one real defect here: the group should be pushed up slightly so white space is more evenly split above and below.

## Page 3
The pipeline diagram (场景 → 攻击 → 防护 → 闭环结果) has unequal node widths: "闭环结果" is noticeably wider than the other three boxes, breaking visual rhythm. The four nodes should have a uniform fixed width. Otherwise clean.

## Page 4
Block pair ("研究意义" / "研究边界") height is mismatched — the right block wraps to two lines forcing it taller while the left is three lines but with a coloured inline span that takes one of those lines; visually the right block appears slightly shorter. Not severe but noticeable. The pipeline above is well-spaced.

## Page 5
Block pair ("已有基础" / "尚未充分回答") height mismatch is real and visible: left block has two bullets that each wrap to a second line (four rendered lines total); right block has two single-line bullets. Left block is noticeably taller. Fix: either add a third bullet right, or constrain left-column text so bullets don't wrap (trim one character per bullet is enough).

## Page 6
The footnote ("PSO 结果是当前参数设置下…") sits between the 说明 block and the footer bar with no visual anchor — it floats in excess white space. Consider placing it flush to the bottom margin or making it a true `\footnote`. The 说明 block text wraps mid-phrase ("动态攻\n击。") with a single character orphaned on line 2 — slight awkwardness.

## Page 7
Block pair ("平台特性" / "记录范围") heights match well. The dashed feedback arrow loops back cleanly. No defects.

## Page 8
The table occupies only ~45% of the slide width and is left-aligned with a wide right void. Either center the table or expand it to ~70% of the text width. The block pair below is height-matched and clean.

## Page 9
Block pair ("步骤" / "攻击类型") is height-mismatched: left has 4 bullets, right has 3, making the right block visibly shorter by about one bullet-height. Add `\vspace` or a phantom line to the right block bottom to equalise.

---

**TOP 2 PRIORITY FIXES**

1. **Page 8 table width** — the narrow left-aligned table with a large right void is the most visually striking layout defect on a data slide; widen or center it.

2. **Page 5 block-pair height mismatch** — the left "已有基础" block is visibly taller than "尚未充分回答"; a committee member glancing at the column layout will notice the ragged bottom edges immediately.

Frametitle wrap\n\nThis is round 4 of iteration. Deck is already in good shape — only flag REAL visual defects that would be noticed by a defense committee. Don't invent issues.\n\nFor each page:\n```\n## Page N\n✅ or 🔴\n[concrete issue + fix]\n```\n\nEnd with TOP 2 priority fixes for these 9 pages. Maximum 400 words."

> TOOL

tool_result Agent
```
## Page 10
✅ Table alignment and footnote styling are clean. Bold min/max values read clearly. No issues.

## Page 11
✅ Four-column table is well-spaced. Red callouts (IMU row) are approved and visually effective. Footnote size and position are consistent with P10.

## Page 12
🔴 **Block-pair height mismatch.** The left block ("观测与奖励") is noticeably taller than the right ("对照设置") because its content wraps to three lines vs. two on the right. The right block sits shorter, leaving visible extra padding at the bottom — the two rounded rectangles do not share a common bottom edge, which looks unbalanced against each other. Fix: add a `\vspace` or dummy line inside the right block, or set both blocks to a fixed `\vbox to <height>` to force equal height.

## Page 13
🔴 **Third-row visual weight is too light.** The 屏蔽诊断维度 row is rendered in a noticeably lighter grey — it reads as disabled/footnote text rather than a real data row. Combined with no alternating-row shading on the other rows, this row looks orphaned. The deliberate de-emphasis may be intentional, but at presentation distance the 13.8% / 100% numbers become nearly illegible. Fix: either raise the grey to ~40% black, or add a brief parenthetical in the same grey at normal weight so the contrast hierarchy is deliberate rather than accidental.

## Page 14
✅ Sparkline axes, dashed threshold line, and two-color curves are clean and readable at presentation scale. Label placement ("失效阈值", "时间") does not crowd the curves. Block-pair heights match well on this page.

## Page 15
✅ Checkmark/X column aligns properly. Red "未抑制" is approved. Two bottom blocks are height-balanced better than P12.

## Page 16
🔴 **Excessive vertical whitespace above "核心结论" block.** There is a large blank band between the frametitle and the first block — roughly one-third of the content area is empty sky. The two lower blocks then look cramped in the bottom half. Fix: reduce `\vspace` above the first block by approximately 0.5–0.8 cm, or raise the entire block group upward so the slide feels vertically centered rather than bottom-heavy.

## Page 17
✅ Block-pair heights match. Bullet spacing inside both columns is even. The large upper whitespace is proportionally larger than P16 but the content area is sparser (3 vs. 2 bullets each), so it reads as intentional breathing room — acceptable.

## Page 18
✅ "谢谢" is centered horizontally and sits in the upper-center with the subtitle and info line below. The bullet-separated info line is clean. No visible separator line artifacts. Vertical center of gravity is slightly high (大字 sits above the midline), which is a common and acceptable Beamer convention for thank-you slides.

---

**TOP 2 PRIORITY FIXES**

1. **P16 — dead space above first block.** The ~30% blank gap between frametitle and content is the most visually jarring defect in this range; it makes the slide look unfinished. Tighten the leading `\vspace` by ~0.6 cm.

2. **P12 — unequal block-pair heights.** The left block's extra wrap line leaves the two rounded rectangles misaligned at the bottom, breaking the visual symmetry that every other block-pair in the deck achieves. Force equal height with `minipage` fixed height or `\vbox to`.

Red on critical failure callouts is intentional.\n\nFor each page output:\n```\n## Page N\n✅ or 🔴\n[only if real issue: concrete fix]\n```\n\nEnd with FINAL VERDICT: ship-ready, or list remaining issues if any. > TOOL

tool_result Agent
```
## Page 1
Title slide. Clean, centered composition. The dark navy title box with white text reads clearly. Author/advisor line and date are well-spaced. The subtle Beamer navigation icons at lower right are standard and unobtrusive.

## Page 2
Outline slide. Four numbered items with good vertical rhythm. The large circle-number bullets in navy are consistent with the theme. Spacing between items is generous and balanced. No issues.

## Page 3
Background motivation slide. The TikZ pipeline (场景 → 攻击 → 防护 → 闭环结果) sits cleanly between the two rounded blocks. Block heights are proportional to their content. The bold underline emphasis on 受控对照 and 防护开启前后 is tasteful and consistent.

Minor observation: there is a noticeable vertical gap between the title bar and the top block — larger than on most other slides. Not necessarily wrong, but slightly airier than peers.

## Page 4
Closed-loop pipeline diagram. Five-box TikZ chain with sublabels below each box is clear. The two-column rounded blocks at the bottom are visually balanced. Bold blue emphasis on 观测一估计一控制 within the left block works well.

## Page 5
Research questions slide. Two-column top section (已有基础 / 尚未充分回答) plus a full-width three-question block at the bottom. Layout is solid. The numbered circles inside the bottom block match the theme. Emphasis colors (blue/red) are consistently applied.

## Page 6
Technical roadmap. The two-row TikZ flow (phases 1-4 on top, 5-7 reversing on bottom) is clean and readable. The explanation block and disclaimer line below it are well-separated. No crowding. The right-angled arrow from 4→5 reads correctly.

## Page 7
Platform architecture slide. The closed-loop TikZ with the dashed feedback arc (CARLA 仿真车辆) is the most visually complex diagram in the deck. The dashed arc curves back cleanly. Two-column blocks below are symmetric and well-proportioned.

## Page 8
Scenario table + two blocks. The booktabs-style table (no vertical rules, clean double-rule top/bottom) is properly spaced. Bold numbers in the 数值 column stand out. The two blocks below are height-matched. One minor point: the table sits quite high with a large white gap above it — a touch more top-margin breathing room than other slides, though consistent with the table-heavy content.

## Page 9
Static attack methodology. Single-column top block (目的) followed by two-column blocks (步骤 / 攻击类型). The numbered list inside 步骤 with colored keyword emphasis (blue PSO, blue terms, red terms) is crisp. Heights of the two bottom blocks are matched.

---

FINAL VERDICT: **Ship-ready.**

All nine pages are visually coherent under the Madrid + whale aesthetic. Block heights, arrow weights, emphasis colors, and frametitle bars are consistent throughout. The only two observations — the slightly larger sub-title gap on page 3 and the tall white space above the table on page 8 — are well within normal Beamer variation and are not defects a committee reviewer would notice. No fixes required.

```
## Page 10
✅ Table-plus-conclusion-block layout is clean. Bold min/max values render correctly. Caption line well-spaced below rule. No vertical crowding.

## Page 11
✅ Red "未抑制" and bold "25.0%" read clearly as critical. Four-column table aligns well. Conclusion block typography matches the deck theme.

## Page 12
🔴 Minor: the TikZ flow diagram sits noticeably high — there is generous white space below the diagram before the two bottom blocks, but very little breathing room between the frametitle bar and the top of the diagram boxes. The vertical rhythm feels top-heavy. The diagram itself is fine aesthetically; it is purely a vertical centering issue. If the diagram were nudged ~4–6pt lower (or the vspace above it increased slightly), the slide would feel more balanced.

## Page 13
✅ Three-row table with bold PPO row reads clearly. Caption and conclusion block well-proportioned. Ample but not excessive white space.

## Page 14
✅ Side-by-side schematic panels are symmetric and well-proportioned. The TikZ curves (blue/red) are legible. The dashed "失效阈值" line is visible. Bottom "共同点" block fills width naturally.

## Page 15
✅ Tick/cross symbols in the second column render crisply. The two bottom side-by-side blocks are height-matched. "5s" bold inline sits correctly within the right block.

## Page 16
🔴 Minor: the two bottom blocks ("方法层面" / "实验层面") sit in the lower third with a large empty band between the top conclusion block and them. The vertical gap between the wide top block and the two lower blocks is noticeably unequal compared to the margins above and below. A modest reduction of that inter-block gap (~8pt) would balance the slide better.

## Page 17
🔴 Minor: the two-column block pair is vertically centered in a large blank field, pushed quite far down the slide. There is roughly twice as much empty space above the blocks as below them (above the footer). This makes the slide look bottom-light. Raising the blocks by ~10–12pt would give more even top/bottom breathing room.

## Page 18
✅ Thank-you slide is clean: large character rendering is sharp, subtitle and affiliation line are well-spaced, white background is intentional and appropriate for a closing slide.

---

FINAL VERDICT: **Conditionally ship-ready.** Three minor vertical-balance issues remain (pages 12, 16, 17) — each is a small spacing tweak, not a structural problem. None would be embarrassing in a live defense; a senior reviewer might notice them but would not object. If you have 15 minutes, nudge the relevant `\vspace` or `\vfill` values on those three slides. Otherwise the deck is presentable as-is.

{

  "content": "# 答辩演讲稿\n\n**篇幅**：约 1500 字 · **预计时长**：5.5–6.5 分钟（按答辩偏慢语速 230–270 字/分计算）\n\n---\n\n## 【P1 · 标题】~15 s\n\n各位老师好。我是王致尧，毕业设计的题目是《自动驾驶汽车信息安全防护的仿真测试研究》，指导教师周纯杰老师。下面汇报研究内容。\n\n## 【P2 · 提纲】~5 s\n\n汇报分研究背景、技术路线、实验结果、总结与展望四个部分。\n\n---\n\n## 【P3 · 研究背景】~25 s\n\n自动驾驶依靠 GNSS 和 IMU 等传感器做定位，而这些传感器都可能被攻击。但定位攻击难以在真实道路上完整验证：攻击会直接干扰车辆运行；反复重放场景的成本太高；真实环境变量多，也不便做受控对照。所以本工作在 CARLA 仿真平台中复现攻击—防护的完整闭环，对比防护开启前后的差异。\n\n## 【P4 · 研究对象】~20 s\n\n研究对象是这条定位—控制闭环：GNSS 和 IMU 的观测进入攻击中间件，送到 EKF 融合，输出位姿给路径跟踪控制器，最终影响车辆轨迹。本文只研究定位链路这一段，不涉及感知和决策。\n\n## 【P5 · 三个研究问题】~30 s\n\n现有研究多在解决\"有没有检测到攻击\"，但告警之后车辆实际是什么状态、是否安全，评估并不充分。本工作具体回答三个问题：第一，不同道路几何下，让车辆失效所需的最小攻击幅度差多少？第二，加入防护以后，静态搜索找到的攻击边界还能反映真实风险吗？第三，会学习的动态攻击者，是否会改变上述评估？\n\n---\n\n## 【P6 · 技术路线】~20 s\n\n为此设计了七个阶段：先建闭环平台和攻击中间件，再做网格扫描和 PSO 搜索找静态攻击的最小有效幅度，加入防护做回放对比；之后用 PPO 训练动态攻击者；最后做结果对比。\n\n## 【P7 · 实验平台】~15 s\n\n平台基于 CARLA 0.9.15 同步仿真。攻击作为可插拔中间件，嵌在传感器输出和 EKF 之间；防护与控制并行运行；传感器、控制指令、攻击和防护事件全程记录，可完整回放。\n\n## 【P8 · 场景与评价指标】~25 s\n\n场景库覆盖 7 张 CARLA 地图、8 类道路模板，共 54 个有效场景实例，仿真步长固定 0.05 秒。严重失效判定为：车辆碰撞、驶出道路，或路径跟踪误差超过 2 米且持续 2 秒。防护评价分检测、拒绝、停车、任务完成四层，每层独立统计。\n\n---\n\n## 【P9 · 静态攻击设计】~20 s\n\n静态攻击考虑三类：GNSS 恒定偏置、GNSS 渐进漂移、IMU 航向偏置。流程是：先在参数空间做网格扫描确定失效区间，再用 PSO 在区间内精细搜索，最后切换不同的定位变体做回放验证。\n\n## 【P10 · 静态攻击结果】~25 s\n\n阈值的分布有一个清晰的规律：**弯道阈值最小，路口阈值最大**，三类攻击的排序完全一致。也就是说，弯道是最脆弱的几何。其中，漂移攻击在不同定位变体之间复现最稳定，作为后续防护评估的输入也最可靠。\n\n## 【P11 · 防护回放】~35 s\n\n把这三组攻击放进开启了防护的环境里重新回放：GNSS 恒定偏置的严重失效率从 15.4% 压到 0；GNSS 渐进漂移从 23.1% 降到 15.4%；但 IMU 航向偏置的 25% 失效率原样保留。原因是卡方和 CUSUM 都是基于 GNSS 残差的统计检测，能挡住 GNSS 类攻击；但 IMU 航向偏置根本不进入它们的视野，所以无法被拒绝。这就回答了第二个问题——**静态参数不能直接代表防护开启后的风险**。\n\n---\n\n## 【P12 · 动态攻击设计】~20 s\n\n为回答第三个问题，让 PPO 在防护开启的环境里训练动态攻击者。观测包括车辆状态、防护统计和攻击历史；奖励同时奖励失效和隐蔽性、惩罚被检测。策略逐步决定攻击幅度，静态搜索结果只用于初始化幅度，不参与决策。\n\n## 【P13 · 动态攻击结果】~30 s\n\n和基线对照比较：无检测惩罚时检测率是 99.4%，攻击全程被发现；PPO 主实验把检测率压到 50.1%，**而严重失效率仍然停在 100%**。也就是说，攻击者学会了在不被持续检测的情况下持续造成失效。3 个随机种子的检测率波动几个百分点，结论方向一致。补充的消融实验把检测率进一步压到 13.8%，验证了隐蔽性确实依赖防护用到的诊断维度。\n\n## 【P14 · 动态攻击行为】~20 s\n\n分析动态攻击行为，可以归纳出两类典型模式：一是持续微小注入，全程低幅扰动让误差缓慢累积；二是隐蔽—爆发切换，前段保持低幅、后段集中扰动。两类策略的共同点是把每一步攻击幅度都压在 CUSUM 累积阈值之下。\n\n## 【P15 · 防护代价】~30 s\n\n把静态和动态结果合起来看：GNSS 类攻击都能被检测、并被显著抑制；但 IMU 航向偏置既不被现有 GNSS 防护覆盖，也无法被自动纠偏。所以**防护检测到攻击，并不等于车辆已经回到安全状态**。我也补充测试了角速率 CUSUM，在 77 次预设 IMU 姿态攻击里都能 5 秒内触发，但它只是补上了告警，车辆控制仍然无法被接管。\n\n---\n\n## 【P16 · 总结】~25 s\n\n核心结论是：评价定位攻击防护不能只看检测率，**检测、拒绝、停车、任务结果必须在同一段闭环里同时记录**。方法层面，本工作搭出了可复现的攻防闭环平台，并提出了四层防护评价方法；实验层面，给出了三类静态攻击在不同道路几何下的候选边界，并量化了静态参数与防护开启后实际风险之间的差距。\n\n## 【P17 · 不足与展望】~15 s\n\n当前工作的不足是：仿真还没引入背景交通流和行人；GNSS 攻击只在软件层注入；防护偏检测，缺少主动恢复。后续希望引入交通流和多种类型的传感器，结合 GNSS 物理层约束做攻击建模，并配合备用定位源研究恢复策略。\n\n## 【P18 · 致谢】~5 s\n\n汇报完毕，恳请各位老师批评指正，谢谢！\n\n---\n\n## 时长校验\n\n| 段落 | 字数 | 时长 (250 字/分) |\n|---|---|---|\n| P1 标题 | 50 | 12 s |\n| P2 提纲 | 25 | 6 s |\n| P3 背景 | 120 | 29 s |\n| P4 研究对象 | 80 | 19 s |\n| P5 三个问题 | 130 | 31 s |\n| P6 技术路线 | 85 | 20 s |\n| P7 平台 | 80 | 19 s |\n| P8 场景与指标 | 115 | 28 s |\n| P9 静态设计 | 80 | 19 s |\n| P10 静态结果 | 90 | 22 s |\n| P11 防护回放 | 165 | 40 s |\n| P12 PPO 设计 | 85 | 20 s |\n| P13 动态结果 | 130 | 31 s |\n| P14 行为模式 | 90 | 22 s |\n| P15 防护代价 | 145 | 35 s |\n| P16 总结 | 120 | 29 s |\n| P17 不足展望 | 90 | 22 s |\n| P18 致谢 | 20 | 5 s |\n| **合计** | **~1600** | **~6.5 min** |\n\n实际答辩中节奏会略慢、过页停顿要算上，整体落在 **6–7 分钟**区间。\n\n## 现场建议\n\n- **关键数字咬清**：54 / 7 / 8 / 0.05 s（P8）；15.4 % → 0 / 23.1 % → 15.4 % / 25 % → 25 %（P11）；99.4 % → 50.1 %（P13）；77 次 / 5 s（P15）。\n- **加重点**：P11 的\"原样保留\"、P13 的\"仍然 100 %\"、P15 的\"检测到 ≠ 已经安全\"——这三处是讲故事的支点。\n- **过场缓冲**：P2 提纲念一句即可翻页；P10 / P11 / P13 / P15 表格出来要给 1–2 秒视觉停顿再开口。\n- **可省备稿**：P6 七步可以略过细节直接念\"七个阶段\"；P9 流程描述可以略过；如果时间紧，先压这两段。\n"
}

---

## Page 1 — Title

✅ Title block proportions and blue rounded rectangle are clean.
🔴 **Vertical dead zone at top.** There is roughly 20% of slide height blank above the title block. The Madrid title-page template pushes content low. Add `\vspace*{-1.2em}` before `\titlepage` or increase the title box's `\vspace` via a `\setbeamertemplate{title page}` adjustment to lift content ~0.8 cm upward.
🔴 **Bottom dead zone.** After the date line, approximately 22% of slide height is empty above the footline. The content cluster occupies only the middle 55% of the canvas. Increase `\vspace{1.5em}` between the subtitle block and author line, or wrap the `\titlepage` in a `\vfill ... \vfill` pair to center the block vertically.

---

## Page 2 — 汇报内容 (TOC)

🔴 **Excessive bottom dead zone.** Item 4 ends at ~62% slide height; the bottom ~28% is empty. The `\vfill` pair enclosing `\tableofcontents` is not effective because the TOC items themselves don't grow. Reduce the `\vspace{1.5em}` in the section-in-toc template from `1.5em` to `1.0em`, and add `\vspace*{1em}` before item 1 to distribute space more evenly. Alternatively, set the TOC to `[shrink=5]` frame option.

---

## Page 3 — 背景与动机

✅ Block pair, bullets, and blue emphases look correct.
🔴 **TikZ pipeline arrow gap.** The four-node pipeline (定位攻击 → 运行时防护 → 车辆控制 → 闭环结果) uses `right=0.45cm` spacing which is tight: the arrow stubs between nodes are barely 2–3 px wide at presentation resolution, making them near-invisible on a projector. Increase to `right=0.7cm` so arrowheads breathe.
🔴 **Bottom block overflow risk.** The 仿真测试价值 block text wraps across two lines and nearly touches the footline (visually less than one line-height gap). Add `\vspace{0.3em}` before the final `\vfill` or increase the lower `\vfill` weighting.

---

## Page 4 — 定位—控制闭环

✅ Two-column block pair (研究意义 / 研究边界) has matched heights — good.
🔴 **TikZ node text spacing issue.** The "EKF 融合定位" node renders the bold "EKF" on one line and "融合定位" on a second line inside a `minimum height=0.95cm` box. The "越界 / 碰撞 \\ 轨迹偏离" node also two-lines. The five nodes are therefore visually inconsistent in text density: single-line nodes (传感链 labels beneath) appear taller than they need to be; two-line nodes look cramped. Increase `minimum height=1.1cm` and add `inner sep=3pt` to the box style to equalize visual weight.
🔴 **Label baseline drift.** The `below=5pt of` label anchor places scriptsize labels at node edge, but the two-line nodes push their bottom edge lower, so "状态估计" and "闭环失效" labels sit lower than "传感链" and "威胁面". Use `below=5pt of <node>.south` consistently or switch to `below=8pt of` to absorb the variation.

---

## Page 5 — 研究目标与主线

🔴 **Block-pair height mismatch.** The 研究基础 (left) block has 2 bullets; 研究目标 (right) has 3 bullets and its third item wraps ("动态检测逃逸" becomes a partial orphan on a second line). At `[T]` alignment this leaves the left block visually shorter by ~1.5 lines. Use `[c]` column alignment or add an explicit `\vspace` inside the left block to equalize.
🔴 **Orphan in right block.** "动态检测逃逸" wraps so that only the blue-bold word appears alone on the continuation line. This is visually distracting against the pale block background. Reflow: shorten the preceding line ("检验防护开启后的" → add a `\mbox{}` or `\linebreak` before "动态") so the bolded term stays on the same line.

---

## Page 6 — 技术路线

✅ Snake-path two-row TikZ is clear; block rendering is correct.
🔴 **Node 7 label truncation.** "7 PPO 动态攻击" is the widest text in the grid but shares `minimum width=2.0cm` with all other nodes. At `\scriptsize` it fits, but the node visually appears slightly wider than its neighbors, breaking grid regularity. Set `minimum width=2.2cm` for s7 only (or globally) to restore uniform column widths.
🔴 **Disclaimer text orphan.** The `\hspace*{1em}{\footnotesize\color{black!55} PSO …}` line is left-aligned (with 1em indent) while all other content is `\centering`. This creates a visual misalignment: the disclaimer floats left beneath a centered block. Wrap it in `\centering` or use `\begin{center}…\end{center}`.

---

## TOP 3 Highest-Impact Fixes

1. **Page 2 TOC bottom dead zone** — reduce `\vspace{1.5em}` to `1.0em` in the `section in toc` template; the lower third of the slide is completely wasted in a title-slide-equivalent page that the audience sees for 30+ seconds.

2. **Page 5 block-pair height mismatch + orphan** — the right block's lone-word wrap on "逃逸" is the most conspicuous single-word orphan across all six pages; fix the column alignment to `[c]` and prevent the wrap with `\mbox{动态检测逃逸}`.

3. **Page 4 TikZ label baseline drift** — inconsistent vertical label positions across a five-node pipeline are immediately visible under projection; standardize to `\node[lab, below=8pt of <node>.south]` for all five labels.

Here is the visual-only design review:

---

## Page 7 — GNSS 创新量检测

✅ Frametitle fits on one line, contrast good.
✅ TikZ flow: arrow weight and node rounding are consistent with the deck's style.
🔴 **Excessive dead space above the flow diagram.** The `\vfill` above the TikZ pushes the diagram about 30% down from the frametitle bar. The combined effect of the top `\vfill`, a spacious `\vfill` between the diagram and the blocks, and a trailing `\vfill` produces three roughly-equal white bands. Remove the topmost `\vfill` (or replace with a fixed `\vspace{0.4em}`) so the content floats higher.
🔴 **Block-pair height imbalance.** The left block holds three display equations with `\scriptsize`; the right block's `C_t > \tau_c \Rightarrow \text{...}` third equation spans two lines due to the inline `\text{本步 GNSS 不更新}` being wider than the column, making the right block taller than the left. Fix: wrap the implication line as two separate `\[...\]` or reduce to `\Rightarrow` on one line and the text on the next with `\quad`.

---

## Page 8 — 实验平台与评价指标

✅ Table rule weights and column spacing are clean.
✅ Block pair is well-balanced in height.
🔴 **Excess gap between table and block pair.** The slide has a large empty strip (~25% of the slide height) between the bottom table rule and the block headers. The `\vfill` before the columns is doing too much work with `\small` table. Replace one of the surrounding `\vfill`s with `\vspace{0.5em}` to tighten.

---

## Page 9 — 静态攻击参数搜索

✅ Full-width goal block on top reads cleanly.
🔴 **Right block ("攻击类型") height-padded with `\vphantom` items.** Two invisible phantom list items are visible as two extra blank bullet rows in the rendered slide — they appear as empty space below "GNSS 渐进漂移" that is slightly misaligned with the left block's four real items. The phantom items produce an unnatural gap. Remove them and instead set `\setlength{\itemsep}` larger, or add a single `\vspace` to match heights more naturally.

---

## Page 10 — PSO 精细搜索

✅ Equation block ("优化目标") and legend table inside it are legible at `\scriptsize`.
🔴 **Interval bar diagram: the red star glyph renders as a font star, not a geometric marker.** The `\node[font=\scriptsize,red!75!black] at (1.55,0) {$\star$}` produces a small math star that is visually undersized relative to the 3pt-thick blue bar and the 1.15pt dots. Replace with `\fill[red!75!black] (1.55,0) circle (2pt)` or a `\node` using `\bigstar` at a larger size so the "candidate minimum" marker is proportionally visible.
🔴 **Bottom block ("搜索过程") has disproportionately large internal white space.** The three-node TikZ chain is small relative to the block's height, leaving obvious padding at the top and bottom of the block. Either reduce the block's inner sep or add a subtitle line to fill it.

---

## Page 11 — 静态攻击搜索结果

✅ Two-column split between interval bars and iteration curve works well.
🔴 **Iteration curve: only x-axis tick labels "1" and "6" are shown** — the intermediate grid lines have no labels. The empty gridline columns between 1 and 6 look like unlabelled dead space. Add `\node` labels at x=2,3,4,5 (even just at `y=-0.10`) so the convergence trajectory can be spatially interpreted.
🔴 **Left block PSO bar for "渐进漂移" is proportionally wrong.** The bar drawn from 1.05 to 2.85 on a 0–5 axis represents ≈21%–57% of the full range, but the actual PSO result (0.021–0.057 m/s) is only 21%–57% of 0.1 m/s (i.e., the axis is 0–0.1). Visually this is not wrong per se, but the label "PSO：0.021–0.057 m/s" overhangs and overlaps the bar's rightmost extent label; add `anchor=east` or shift it left by ~0.3 cm.

---

## Page 12 — PPO 动态攻击建模

✅ Four-node loop diagram is clean and symmetrical.
✅ Left/right block height difference is acceptable (narrow left, wide right with math).
🔴 **Reward equation line-break is visually awkward.** The `r_t = R_f\,\mathbf{1}[t=T]\,f_T - \lambda_a\lVert a_t\rVert_2 - \lambda_d d_t` is forced onto two display lines in the slide render, breaking after `f_T`. This creates an unbalanced split (long first line, two short terms on the second). Use `\begin{aligned}` to intentionally break at both minus signs into three balanced lines, or use `multline` to center the continuation.

---

## TOP 3 Highest-Impact Fixes

1. **P7 top whitespace + CUSUM equation overflow (highest priority).** The dead space above the flow diagram, combined with the right-block text overrun, makes the slide look structurally loose. Fix the top `\vfill` → `\vspace{0.4em}` and wrap the CUSUM implication to two lines.

2. **P11 iteration curve x-axis labels.** Missing tick labels at iterations 2–5 make the convergence curve unreadable — viewers cannot tell how fast J drops. Add four `\node` labels; this is a one-line TikZ addition per tick.

3. **P9 phantom list items in "攻击类型" block.** The two `\vphantom` hack lines are visible as unexplained blank bullet indentations in the rendered slide. Remove them and use a `\vspace` instead — cleaner and non-distracting.

```
## Page 13 — PPO 训练结果

🔴 **Vertical dead space above the table.** There is a large empty band (~30% of slide height) between the frametitle bar and the table's toprule. The table sits in the middle third, leaving the top quarter completely blank. The block at the bottom is well-positioned, but the overall vertical distribution is bottom-heavy. Fix: replace `\vfill` above the table with `\vspace{1.2em}` so the table rises closer to the title bar and the gap between table and block becomes uniform.

🔴 **"严重失效率" column header is wide, forcing narrow column spacing.** The four-column layout compresses the "备注" column, causing the right-hand cell "检测率降至 50.1%，失效率仍为 100%" (P15 analogous row) to risk overflow. On P13, "攻击全程被检测" wraps awkwardly if the slide is projected narrower. Fix: abbreviate the column header to "严重失效" or add `\shortstack` line-break inside the header.

✅ Footnote color (`black!70`, `\footnotesize`) is appropriately recessed. Booktabs rules are correct weight. Bold on the PPO row reads clearly.

---

## Page 14 — 策略行为分析

✅ The two-column sparkline layout is balanced and the block-pair fills the upper half cleanly.

🔴 **Axis arrow weight (0.6 pt) is lighter than the data lines (0.7 pt).** At projection size the axes visually recede while data lines dominate — acceptable — but the axis arrowheads are tiny and the y-axis has no tick or label, making it ambiguous whether the y-scale is the same in both panels. A single `\tiny` "幅度" rotated label on the left panel's y-axis would anchor the reader without adding clutter.

🔴 **"失效阈值" label on both panels sits flush against the top of the dashed line with only `xshift=3pt`.** At projection resolution the label clips against the top of the TikZ bounding box in the right panel (where the red line rises above y=1.0). The label is partially obscured. Fix: change `above` to `right` and remove the xshift, or shrink `y=1.0cm` slightly to give headroom.

---

## Page 15 — 攻击评估流程

🔴 **Last table row overflows its column.** The "产出" cell "检测率降至 50.1%，失效率仍为 100%" is visibly compressed and runs close to the right margin with `@{}lll@{}` and no fixed column width. At actual projection width the text is at risk of clipping. Fix: set the third column as `p{5cm}` or `>{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{4.8cm}` to allow controlled wrapping.

🔴 **Large vertical gap between table bottom and the two column blocks below.** Two `\vfill` tokens create an uneven distribution: the gap above the table is larger than the gap between table and blocks. The blocks are pushed toward the bottom third. Fix: replace the middle `\vfill` with `\vspace{0.9em}` to anchor the table higher and give the blocks more breathing room.

✅ The `lll` column alignment is consistent and the toprule/midrule/bottomrule cadence is clean.

---

## Page 16 — 工作总结

✅ The wide top block plus two equal column blocks is structurally solid and the hierarchy reads correctly.

🔴 **Left block (方法层面) has 3 bullet items, right block (实验层面) has 2.** The left block is taller by roughly one line, causing a visible height mismatch between the two columns. The right block's bottom edge sits noticeably higher. Fix: add `\vspace{0.28em}` after the last item in the right block, or restructure to match item counts.

---

## Page 17 — 不足与展望

🔴 **Severe vertical centering imbalance.** The two column blocks occupy less than half the slide height and are vertically centered in the lower-center zone, leaving the entire upper third of the slide (below the frametitle) empty. The `\vspace{1.2em}` pushes the blocks down without balancing from above. Fix: change to `\vspace{0.4em}` (or use `\vfill` above and below the columns together) to distribute whitespace evenly above and below.

✅ The left block (4 items) and right block (3 items) height difference is minor; the `\itemsep{0.28em}` partially compensates for the count difference and the mismatch is not distracting.

---

## Page 18 — 谢谢

✅ The 52 pt bold "谢\;\;谢" with double kern `\;` is well-executed — letter spacing reads as intentional and elegant, not forced.

🔴 **The three-segment info line ("王致尧 · 华中科技大学... · 2026年5月") uses `\textbullet` as separator with `\;` spacing on each side.** At render size the bullet sits slightly low relative to the CJK baseline, making the separator look typographically inconsistent with the surrounding text. Fix: replace `\;\textbullet\;` with `\enspace\cdot\enspace` or `\;{\normalfont·}\;` to use a middle dot (U+00B7) that sits at the correct CJK punctuation height.

✅ No frametitle bar on P18 is consistent with a full-bleed closing slide. Vertical centering via double `\vfill` is correct.

---

## TOP 3 Highest-Impact Fixes

1. **P17 dead whitespace (highest priority).** The limitations slide looks unfinished — two compact blocks floating in the bottom half of an otherwise empty slide. Reduce `\vspace{1.2em}` to `\vspace{0.4em}` and wrap the columns in `\vfill...\vfill` to center them vertically. This is the most visually jarring issue across all six slides.

2. **P15 last-row "产出" cell overflow.** The long text string in the PPO row will clip or wrap erratically on projectors with lower DPI scaling. Change the third column spec to `>{\raggedright\arraybackslash}p{4.8cm}` — a one-character fix with the highest failure risk if left unfixed.

3. **P14 "失效阈值" label clipping in right panel.** The threshold label is partially occluded by the red trajectory line crossing y=1.0 in the burst panel. Switch the node anchor from `above` to `right` to move it clear of the data line. This is the only defect where live projection will actively mislead the audience's eye.

Red is approved for failure-state callouts.\n\nR1 already fixed: TOC spacing, P3 arrow gaps, P5 wrap, P6 disclaimer alignment, P7 CUSUM line break, P9 vphantom hack, P10 star marker, P11 axis ticks.\n\n**Round 2 — only flag REMAINING visible defects:**\n1. Frametitle wraps\n2. Whitespace balance\n3. TikZ aesthetic (node sizing, arrow paths, label positioning, CJK letter-spacing)\n4. Block-pair height equality\n5. Math display rendering\n6. Bullet wrap\n7. P10 has 4 nested blocks (优化目标 + 搜索过程 left, 从区间到候选值 right) — does the LEFT column structure (2 stacked blocks) look balanced vs right (1 tall block)?\n8. P11 — convergence chart ticks now show 1-6, but does \"6\" tick label collide with \"迭代轮次\" axis label?\n\nFor each page:\n```\n## Page N — frametitle\n✅ or 🔴\n[concrete issue + fix]\n```\n\nEnd with TOP 2-3 highest-impact fixes. Maximum 500 words. Be precise. Do not invent issues."

> TOOL

tool_result Agent
```
## Page 1 — 自动驾驶汽车信息安全防护的仿真测试研究
✅ Title slide is clean. The blue banner renders the full title on one line. Author/advisor/date vertical spacing is generous but appropriate for a title card. No issues.

## Page 2 — 汇报内容 (TOC)
🔴 The four items are vertically distributed across roughly 70% of the content area but the bottom ~30% is dead white space. The gap below item 4 "总结与展望" to the footer is noticeably larger than the gap between items. The top gap (below frametitle) is also larger than between items. Fix: reduce `\vspace` above item 1 or add a small `\vfill` after item 4 to pull items closer to center-of-content-area, or add a thin decorative rule below item 4.

## Page 3 — 背景与动机
🔴 The bottom block "仿真测试价值" has its body text wrap to two lines. The second line ("景，比较**攻击强度**、**防护触发**和**车辆失效结果**。") ends mid-width, leaving the block about 40% empty on the right of line 2. This is fine for content, but the block sits visually low — there is roughly equal padding above the disclaimer line ("PSO 结果…") and the footer, making the disclaimer look like orphaned text. That disclaimer is absent on P3, so no issue there. The arrow chain (4 nodes) is well-spaced.
✅ Block heights: single "攻击危害性" block (top) and single "仿真测试价值" block (bottom) are not paired, so height equality is not applicable. Overall acceptable.

## Page 4 — 定位一控制闭环
✅ The 5-node arrow chain renders cleanly with even spacing. The two bottom blocks "研究意义" and "研究边界" appear height-matched. Frametitle single line. The large whitespace gap between the arrow chain and the two blocks (~15% of content height) is somewhat empty but serves as visual breathing room. Acceptable.

## Page 5 — 研究目标与主线
🔴 **Block-pair height imbalance**: The top row has "研究基础" (2 bullets) left and "研究目标" (3 bullets) right. The right block is taller by approximately one bullet height, causing visible misalignment at the bottom edge. Fix: either add a `\vphantom{}` line or `\strut` spacer inside "研究基础", or use `\begin{columns}[t]` with explicit equal-height via a minipage trick to align tops instead of bottoms.
✅ Frametitle fits on one line. "本文工作主线" full-width block looks fine.

## Page 6 — 技术路线
✅ The two-row arrow diagram is clean and well-spaced. "说明" block fills the width appropriately. The disclaimer text below sits with reasonable separation. No wrap issues, no frametitle wrap.

## Page 7 — GNSS 创新量检测
🔴 **Left/right block content height mismatch**: "创新量与卡方统计量" has 3 math equations; "CUSUM 累积统计量" has 3 lines but one is a plain-text conclusion (C_t > τ_c ⇒ GNSS 不更新). The left block appears slightly taller. More critically, the **large blank region between the 5-node arrow chain and the two math blocks** (roughly 12% of the slide height) feels excessive — the arrow chain sits isolated in the upper third with no visual bridge to the blocks below. Fix: reduce `\vspace` between arrow chain and blocks, or move the chain slightly lower.
✅ Frametitle "GNSS 创新量检测" fits on one line with comfortable margin.

## Page 8 — 实验平台与评价指标
🔴 **Block-pair height imbalance**: "严重失效判定" (left, 2 lines) is visibly shorter than "防护四层指标" (right, 3 lines). The left block's bottom edge sits ~one text line higher than the right block. This is the most noticeable paired-block height mismatch in the deck. Fix: add `\\[0.5ex]` or a `\vphantom{任务完成四项}` spacer line inside the left block body.
✅ Table formatting is clean, booktabs-style rules visible.

## Page 9 — 静态攻击参数搜索
🔴 **Right column height vs left**: "步骤" (left, 4 bullets) is taller than "攻击类型" (right, 2 bullets). The right block has substantial dead space at the bottom. This is a known structural imbalance. Fix: either add a short explanatory line to "攻击类型" (e.g., "覆盖偏置与漂移两类扰动模式") or reduce the left block to 3 bullets by merging bullets 1–2.

---

## TOP 3 Highest-Impact Fixes

1. **P8 block-pair height** — "严重失效判定" vs "防护四层指标" is the most visually jarring mismatch. Add a phantom line or explicit vspace inside the shorter left block. Single-line fix, high visual payoff.

2. **P5 top block-pair height** — "研究基础" (2 items) sitting shorter than "研究目标" (3 items) undermines a structurally important slide. Use `[t]`-aligned columns with a `\vphantom` row in the left block to equalize.

3. **P9 right-column emptiness** — "攻击类型" with only 2 bullets against "步骤"'s 4 bullets leaves ~40% dead space in the right block. Add one sentence of explanatory content or explicitly set both columns to `[T]` with a fixed height so the disparity reads as intentional padding rather than an accident.

```
## Page 10 — PSO 精细搜索
🔴 Left column vertical imbalance: "优化目标" block is significantly taller than "搜索过程" — and below 搜索过程 there is a large dead zone of ~30% of slide height. The two left blocks together are shorter than the single right block "从区间到候选值", creating misaligned bottom edges. Fix: add `\vfill` between the two left blocks or increase the 搜索过程 block's inner padding so both columns terminate at the same vertical level.

## Page 11 — 静态攻击搜索结果
🔴 The x-axis tick "5" is partially obscured — the "迭代轮次" axis label overlaps with or crowds tick "5" on the right. The label sits at the same height as the tick numeral, visually merging. Fix: push the axis label slightly further right (e.g. `right=2pt` after the last tick), or move label below the axis end arrow rather than inline.

🔴 The bottom caption "展示内容：网格扫描确定候选范围，PSO 给出低幅度有效参数。" is redundant — the right block already contains the caption "PSO 在候选范围内继续压低攻击代价。" and the table on P10 already shows the numbers. The standalone line adds no new information and creates an odd floating text region below the columns. Remove it.

## Page 12 — PPO 动态攻击建模
✅ Reward equation multi-line alignment is clean and readable. Vertical balance between the flow diagram and the two lower blocks is fine.

## Page 13 — PPO 训练结果
🔴 Large empty space: the table and footnote occupy the top ~40% of the content area; the "训练结果" callout block sits in the middle; the bottom ~30% of the slide is blank white. The layout reads as top-heavy with wasted space below the callout. Fix: wrap both elements in a `minipage` or `vspace` structure so the pair is vertically centered in the frame, or expand the callout block to fill additional height.

## Page 14 — 策略行为分析
✅ 失效阈值 labels are visible and cleanly positioned at right edge of both sparklines. No collision. Layout balanced.

## Page 15 — 攻击评估流程
🔴 Last table row "PPO 训练" wraps "检测率降至 50.1%，失效率仍 / 100%" across two lines while all other rows are single-line. This creates an uneven row height that makes the table look ragged and emphasizes an orphan "100%" on its own line. Fix: either shorten the cell text to fit one line (e.g. "检测率降至 50.1%；失效率仍 100%") or widen the `产出` column slightly.

## Page 16 — 工作总结
✅ Left 3 bullets vs right 3 bullets — block heights are visually matched. No overflow.

## Page 17 — 不足与展望
✅ Blocks are vertically centered in the frame. The 4-bullet left vs 3-bullet right slight height difference is acceptable given content.

## Page 18 — 谢谢
✅ The middle dot (·) between name, institution, and date renders as a clean full-width CJK interpunct. No collision with surrounding text.

---

## Top 3 Highest-Impact Fixes

1. **P15 table last-row wrap** — "100%" orphaned on its own line is the most visually jarring defect; shorten the cell text so the row is single-line like all others.

2. **P11 caption removal** — The free-floating "展示内容：..." line below the columns is redundant noise that degrades the clean two-column layout; delete it.

3. **P10 left-column bottom gap** — The ~30% dead space below "搜索过程" while the right column "从区间到候选值" extends further down breaks the visual frame alignment; equalize column heights with `\vfill` or matched `minipage` heights.

Red is approved for failure callouts.\n\nTwo prior rounds fixed major issues. Round 3 — only flag REMAINING visible defects worth fixing. Don't invent issues. If a page is clean, say so.\n\nFor each page:\n```\n## Page N — frametitle\n✅ or 🔴\n[concrete issue + concrete fix]\n```\n\nEnd with TOP 2 highest-impact fixes. Maximum 400 words."

```
## Page 1 — Title slide
✅ Clean. Title block, author line, institution, and date are well-spaced. No defects.

## Page 2 — 汇报内容
🔴 The four TOC items sit in a large white void — too much vertical whitespace between them relative to the slide height. The items feel adrift rather than anchored. Fix: reduce `\vspace` or `\itemsep` between items, or add a thin horizontal rule below the frametitle to visually anchor the list to the top.

## Page 3 — 背景与动机
✅ Clean. Two blocks, pipeline diagram, and red emphasis text are all well-proportioned.

## Page 4 — 定位一控制闭环
🔴 The two lower blocks (研究意义 / 研究边界) have noticeably more bottom padding than the pipeline diagram above — the diagram-to-block gap is larger than the block-to-slide-bottom gap, making the layout feel top-heavy. Fix: add a small `\vspace` between the pipeline row and the two blocks, or slightly shrink the gap between the frametitle rule and the pipeline to redistribute vertical whitespace more evenly.

## Page 5 — 研究目标与主线
✅ Clean. Three-block layout is balanced, bullet emphasis is consistent.

## Page 6 — 技术路线
🔴 The caveat line "PSO 结果是当前参数设置下的候选最优，不代表全局最优。" sits as bare small-font text below the 说明 block, visually orphaned at the bottom of the slide. It reads like a footnote dropped into body space. Fix: either place it inside the 说明 block as a second sentence, or format it as a true `\footnotesize` footnote anchored to the slide bottom (e.g., via `\vfill` + rule).

## Page 7 — GNSS 创新量检测
✅ Clean. Pipeline, two formula blocks, and equation layout are well-balanced.

## Page 8 — 实验平台与评价指标
✅ Clean. Table and two lower blocks are proportioned well.

## Page 9 — 静态攻击参数搜索
✅ Clean. Block layout, numbered bullets, and blue emphasis text are consistent.

---

**TOP 2 highest-impact fixes:**

1. **Page 6 — orphaned caveat line.** The bare italic disclaimer below the 说明 block is the most visually jarring element across all nine slides. Move it inside the block or anchor it as a proper bottom footnote.

2. **Page 2 — TOC vertical whitespace.** Four items scattered across a nearly empty slide looks unfinished. Tightening inter-item spacing (or adding a top rule) costs one line of TeX and immediately makes the slide look purposeful.

```
## Page 10 — PSO 精细搜索

🔴 **Column bottom misalignment confirmed.** The left column has two stacked blocks (优化目标 + 搜索过程). The 搜索过程 block ends noticeably higher than the bottom of the right column (从区间到候选值), leaving a visible gap. The right column's table fills more vertical space and terminates lower. Fix: add `\vfill` or a fixed `\vspace` after the 搜索过程 block, or set both columns to `[t]` alignment with equal explicit heights using a `\vbox to <height>{}` wrapper so bottoms register.

## Page 11 — 静态攻击搜索结果

✅ Clean. Left column range-bars and right convergence plot are well-balanced. No defects.

## Page 12 — PPO 动态攻击建模

✅ Clean. Flow diagram and two lower blocks are well proportioned.

## Page 13 — PPO 训练结果

🔴 **Excessive vertical whitespace above the table.** The table sits roughly in the vertical center of the slide leaving ~40% of the slide body empty above it before content starts. The 训练结果 callout block at the bottom is well-placed, but the top half of the slide feels hollow. Fix: remove or reduce the leading `\vspace` / `\bigskip` before the tabular so the table rises to sit closer under the frametitle, matching the density of surrounding slides.

## Page 14 — 策略行为分析

✅ Clean. The two chart panels and 共同点 block fill the frame evenly with no obvious gaps or misalignments.

## Page 15 — 攻击评估流程

✅ Last-row 100% orphan is gone. The table reads correctly with four complete rows. The two bottom blocks (分工 / 评价口径) align well. No defects.

## Page 16 — 工作总结

✅ Clean. Three-block layout (full-width top + two-column bottom) is proportionate.

## Page 17 — 不足与展望

🔴 **Bottom-half near-empty.** The two columns (当前不足 / 后续工作) occupy only the upper ~45% of the slide body; the lower half is blank. It is the most under-filled slide in the deck. Fix: either increase the block font size by one step (`\large`), add a third row of content, or stretch the blocks with `minimum height` in the tcolorbox options to occupy the lower region.

## Page 18 — 谢谢

✅ Clean closing slide. Typography, spacing, and subtitle line are all appropriate.

---

**TOP 2 Highest-Impact Fixes**

1. **P13 — Remove excess top whitespace.** The PPO training table floats with ~40% blank slide-body above it, which reads as a layout error to the committee. Reducing/eliminating the leading vspace before the table fixes the most jarring emptiness in the deck.

2. **P17 — Fill the lower half of 不足与展望.** The two columns leave the bottom 55% of the slide body blank — the starkest under-density issue across pages 10–18. Stretching the blocks or enlarging text to fill the space is the single biggest visual improvement remaining.
